“tiripuvaṉac cakkaravarttikaḷ srī kulōttuṅka cōḻa tēvaṟku yāṇṭu nāṟpattāṟāvatu
rājātirāja vaḷanāṭṭut taṉiyūr perumpaṟṟappuliyūr uṭiyār tirucciṟṟampala
muṭiyārkkut tirunantavaṉappuṟamākavum srīmākēsvararkkut tiruvamutu ceyya maṭappuṟam nam
perumāḷ tiruttaṅkiyār maturāntakiyāḻvār vācciyaṉ iravitirucciṟṟampala muṭiyāṉ
pēril vili koṇṭa nilam kiṭāraṅkoṇṭa cōḻap pēriḷami nāṭṭu erukkaṭṭāñ cēriyāṉa
jayaṅkoṇṭa cōḻa nallūrp pāl" (te. i. ka. tokuti 4 eṇ 222) eṉavarum kalveṭṭup
pakutiyil kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām.
iṅku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya kalveṭṭup pakutikaḷāl mutaṟ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ taṅkimārākiya
irācarācaṉ kuntaviyārum, maturāntaki yāḻvārum tillip peruṅkōyilukkut
tiruppaṇikaḷ palapurintuḷḷami naṉku puvaṉātal kāṇalām.
mutaṟkulōttuṅka cōḻaṉatu nāṟpattāṟām āṭciyāṇṭil toṇṭi 'nāṭṭuk kārikik kuḷattūr
talivaṉum, miḻali nāṭṭu vēḷāṇmi koṇṭavaṉumākiya kaṇṭaṉ mātavaṉ eṉpāṉ tilliyampalattiṉ
vaṭakīḻt ticiyil coṉṉavāṟṟivār kōyilum civapurāṇaṅkaḷi virittuk kūṟutaṟku
iṭamākiya purāṇa maṇṭapamum ataṉi yoṭṭiya tirumāḷikip pattiyum malipōṉṟu uyarttu
tōṉṟa variciyākak kaṭṭiṉāṉ. icceyti nīṭūrk kōyilil variyap peṟṟuḷḷa kalveṭṭil,
"āriyavulakam aṉittiyum kuṭikkīḻ
      ākkiya kulōttuṅka cōḻaṟ
kāṇṭoru nāṟpattāriṭit tilli.
      pampalattē vaṭa kīḻpāl
pōriyal matattuc coṉṉavāṟṟivār
      kōyilum purāṇa nūl virikkum
purici māḷikiyum variciyā viḷaṅkap
      poruppiṉāṉ viruppuṟac ceytāṉ ... kaṇṭaṉ --- mātavaṉē"
eṉa varum pāṭalil viritturikkap peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām ippāṭalil coṉṉavāṟaṟivār kōyil
eṉṟatu, yārum yāvum kaḻaṟiṉavum aṟiyum āṟṟali iṟivaṉaruḷāṟ peṟṟa cēramāṉ
perumāḷukku amitta tirukkōyilākum. tillip peruṅkōyiliṉ vaṭakīḻtticiyiṟ kaṇṭaṉ
mātavaṉāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa ittirukkōyil kālappaḻamiyāl pēṇuvāriṉṟic citintu aḻintu
pōyviṭṭateṉak karuta vēṇṭiyuḷatu. tillip peruṅkōyilil kūttap perumāṉic
cuṟṟiyuḷḷa mutal tiruccuṟṟu vikkirama cōḻaṉ tirumāḷiki eṉavum; iraṇṭām
tiruccuṟṟu kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ tirumāḷiki eṉavum, mūṉṟām tiruccuṟṟu, irācākkaḷ
tampirāṉ tiruvīti eṉavum kalveṭṭukkaḷiṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ
tirumāḷiṉakayākiya iraṇṭām tiruccuṟṟiṉ mēṟpuṟattil aminta kōpuravāyil kulōttuṅka
cōḻaṉ tirumāḷikip puṟavāyil (te.i.ka. to, eṇ, -22) eṉak kalveṭṭiṟ
kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.
vācciyaṉ tirucciṟṟampalamuṭiyāṉ caṅkaraṉāṉa teṉṉavaṉ piraparāyaṉ eṉpāṉ,
perumpaṟṟappuliyūr vaṭapiṭāki maṇalūril tāṉ aṉupavittu varukiṟa maṇaṟ kolli kāl
vēli nilattiyum cuṅkat tavirttaruḷiṉa kulōttuṅka cōḻa tēvar makaḷār ammaṅkiyāḻvārāṉa
periya nācciyārkkuc cēmamāka cammatittu aṉṉiya nāma karaṇattāl tillip
peruṅkōyilukku aḷittuḷḷāṉ. (te.i.ka.toruti IV eṇ 226), aṭuttu.,
"tirumaṇi poṟṟōṭ ṭeḻutu pattāṇṭil
varutiṟi muṉṉē maṉṉavar cumantu
tiṟi niṟittuc corinta cempoṟkuviyāl
taṉkula nāyakaṉ tāṇṭavam payilum
cempoṉampalañ cūḻ tirumāḷikiyum
kōpuravācal kūṭacālikaḷum
ulaku valaṅkoṇ ṭoḷiviḷaṅku nēmik
kulavaṉāyutaya kuṉṟamoṭu niṉṟeṉap
pacumpoṉ vēynta palivaḷar pīṭamum
vicumpoḷi taḻippa viḷaṅku poṉ vēyntu
irunilan taḻippa imiyavar kaḷippa
periya tirunāḷ perumpeyar viḻāveṉum
uyar pūraṭṭāti uttiraṭṭātiyil
ampalam niṟinta aṟputakkūttar
impar vāḻa eḻuntaruḷuvataṟkut
tiruttērk kōyil cempoṉ vēyntu
paruttiraṉ muttiṉ payilvaṭam parappi
niṟimaṇi māḷiki neṭuntiruvītitaṉ
tiruvaḷar peyarāṟ ceytu camittaruḷi
pimpoṟ kuḻitta parikala mutalāc
cempoṟ kaṟpakattoṭu paricciṉṉamum
aḷavillātaṉa voḷipeṟa amittup
pattāmāṇṭiṟ cittirit tiṅkaḷ
attam peṟṟa āti vārattut
tiruvaḷar matiyiṉ tiriyōtacippakkattu
iṉṉa palavum iṉitu camittaruḷi"
eṉavarum vikkirama cōḻaṉiṉ meykkīrttip pakuti avaṉ ceyta tillit tiruppaṇikaḷit
teḷivāka eṭutturikkiṉṟatu.
"aḻakiya maṇipatitta poṉṉēṭṭiṟ kuṟippiṭṭu eḻutattakka taṉatu pattām āṭciyāṇṭil
taṉakkuk kīḻppaṭinta maṉṉavarkaḷ cumantu vantu niṟitta tiṟipporuḷākiya poṟkuviyiṉik
koṇṭu taṉ kulateyvamākiya civaperumāṉ tirunaṭam puriyum poṉṉampalattiṉic cūḻavuḷḷa
cuṟṟu māḷikiyum ataṉiyaṭuttuḷḷa kōpuravāyilum malikaḷum ulakiṉic cūḻavuḷḷa
cakkaravāḷa mali katiravaṉ utikkum utaya maliyuṭaṉ kūṭi niṉṟāṟ pōla poṉṉāl vēyappaṭṭa
palipīṭamum vāṉattil viḷaṅkap poṉ vēyntu maṇṇulakattavar viṇṇavar makiḻattāṉ piṟanta
periyaviḻā veṉṉumpūraṭṭāti, - uttiraṭṭāti tirucciṟṟampalattiṉ aruḷum
vāyniṟintu āṭum aṟputak kūttiṉi nikaḻtti yaruḷum cuttapperumāṉ ivvulakattavar
vāḻa eḻuntaruḷit tiruvulā varutaṟkuriya iyaṅkut tirukkōyilākiya tiruttēriṉic
cempoṟ kūriyuṭiyatākap poṉ vēyntu ataṉ kaṇ parumi vāynta muttu vaṭaṅkaḷi
variciyākat toṅkumpaṭi aṇi ceytu, attērāṉatu ulā varutaṟku ēṟṟavāṟu
tillippatiyil niṟimaṇi māḷikiyuṭiya neṭun tiruvītikaḷāka nāṉku vītikaḷiyum
vikkirama cōḻaṉ tiruvīti eṉat taṉ peyarāl amittup paciyapoṉṉiṉāl uṭkuḻivuṭiyatākac
ceyyap peṟṟa parikalam (uṇkalam) mutalākac cem poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa kaṟpakat taruviṉiyum
paricciṉṉaṅkaḷiyum aḷavillātaṉavāka oḷi peṟa amittut taṉatu pattām āṭciyāṇṭil
cittiri mātam atta viṇmīṉuṭaṉ kūṭiya ñāyiṟṟuk-kiḻamiyil vaḷarpiṟit tirayōtacit
titiyil ittakiya pala tiruppaṇikaḷit tillip peruṅkōyiliṟ ceytu niṟivēṟṟiṉāṉ"
eṉpatu mēṟkuṟitta meykkīrtti kūṟum ceytiyākum.
ivvāṟu vikkirama cōḻaṉ tillippatiyiṟ ceyta tiruppaṇikaḷ yāvum ki.pi. 1128 ām
āṇṭu ēppiral mātam patiṉintām nāḷil niṟivēṟiṉa eṉpatu immeyk kīrttiyiṟ
kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kālakkuṟippiṉāl naṉku aṟiyappaṭum. tillicciṟṟampalattic cūḻavuḷḷa
tirumāḷikip pattiyākiya mutaṟpirakāram ivvēntaṉāl amikkap peṟṟamiyiṉ “vikkirama
cōḻaṉ tirumāḷiki” eṉa vaḻaṅkiyateṉpatu kalveṭṭukaḷāṟ pulaṉākiṉṟatu. (Ins, No. 282,
284; and 287 of 1913).
vikkiramacōḻaṉ perum paṟṟappuliyūrākiya tilliyiṟ peruvītiyamitta ceytiyik kavic
cakkaravartti oṭṭak kūttar ivaṉ makaṉ iraṇṭāṅkulōttuṅkaṉik kuṟittup pāṭiya kulōttuṅka
cōḻaṉ piḷḷittamiḻil,
'pāvaka nirampu tiru mālum malarōṉum
      paranta patiṉeṇ kaṇamum vantu paravantam
cēvaka nirampu tiruvīti puliyūriṟ
      ceyta perumāṉ
      (kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ piḷḷittamiḻ 9-7)
eṉavarum toṭariṟ pārāṭṭip pōṟṟiyuḷḷami kāṇalām.
tillit tirukkōyiliṟ civakaṅkit tīrttattiṟku mēṟpakkattēyuḷḷa nūṟṟukkāl maṇṭapam
vikkirama cōḻaṉ paṇitta vaṇṇam avaṉuṭiya paṭit talivaṉākiya arumpākkiḻāṉ maṇaviṟ kūttaṉ
kāliṅkarāyaṉ eṉpavaṉāṟ kaṭṭappeṟṟatākum. immaṇṭapattil paṉṉiraṇṭu tūṇkaḷil
‘vikkiramacōḻaṉ tiru maṇṭapam' eṉappeyarpoṟikkap peṟṟiruttalāl immaṇṭapam vikkirama
cōḻaṉ peyarāl avaṉatu paṭittalivaṉ kāliṅkarāyaṉāṟ kaṭṭap peṟṟateṉat terikiṟatu.
immaṇṭapattik kaṭṭiyavaṉ arumpākkiḻāṉ maṇaviṟkūttaṉ kāliṅkarāyaṉ eṉpatu
"mallaṟ kulavariyāl nūṟṟukkāl maṇṭapattit
tillip pirāṉukkuc ceytamittāṉ - kollam
aḻivukaṇṭāṉ cēraṉ aḷappariya vāṟṟaṟ
kiḻivu kaṇṭāṉ toṇṭipā rēṟu” -
      (te. i. ka. to . IV pak. 33)
eṉavarum citamparam kalveṭṭuc ceyyuḷāl aṟiyappaṭum.
tilliyampalavāṇar mācimaka nāḷil kiḷḷiyiluḷḷa kaṭal tuṟiyil tīrttamāṭi
vīṟṟiruntaruḷutaṟku maṇṭapamum, tilliyiliruntu kiḷḷik kaṭaṟkarikkuc cellutaṟkuriya
peruvaḻiyum vikkirama cōḻaṉ āṭciyil avvēntaṉatu āṇi eṅkum nikaḻa avaṉ
paṭittalivaṉākiya kāliṅkarāyaṉāl amikkap peṟṟaṉa. icceyti,
“mācik kaṭalāṭi vīṟṟirukka maṇṭapamum
pēcaṟṟa aṟṟip peruvaḻiyum - īcaṟkut
teṉpuliyūrk kēyamittāṉ kūttaṉ ticiyaṉittum
maṉpuliyāṇi naṭakka vantu"
      (te. i. ka. to IV. pak, 34)
eṉavarum veṇpāvāl iṉitu pulaṉātal kāṇalām.
arumpākkiḻāṉ kāliṅkarāṉākiya ivaṉ mutaṟkulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ āṭciyilum vikkirama cōḻaṉ
āṭciyilum paṭit talivaṉāka viḷaṅkiyavaṉ. ivaṉ toṇṭi maṇṭalattu irupattu nāṉku
kōṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya maṇaviṟ kōṭṭattiṉ taliyūrākiya maṇavil eṉṉum ūriṉaṉ. toṇṭiyar
kōṉākiya ittalivaṉ mutaṟkulōttuṅkaṉatu āṭciyil, paṭittalivaṉāyamarntu vēṇāṭu
maḻināṭu pāṇṭināṭu vaṭanāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟil nikaḻnta pōril taṉ vēntaṉukta veṟṟiyi
nalkip perumpukaḻ peṟṟāṉ. ivaṉatu veṟṟic ceyalkaḷi naṉkuṇarnta mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ
ivaṉukkuk kāliṅkarāyaṉ, eṉṉum paṭṭamaḷittup pārāṭṭiṉāṉ. ivaṉ aruḷākaraṉ,
arumpākkimāṉ poṉṉampalak kūttaṉ, naralōka vīraṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷāṟ pārāṭṭap
peṟṟuḷḷāṉ. ivaṉ vikkiramacōḻaṉ āṭciyiṉ muṟpakutiyilum paṭit talivaṉāka iruntaṉaṉ
eṉpatu oṭṭakkūttar pāṭiya vikkirama cōḻaṉulāvāl naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu.
cōḻa vēntarkku veṟṟi viḷikkum pōrttiṟam vāynta . kaliṅkarāyaṉākiya ittalivaṉ tamiḻ
makkaḷ uḷḷattilē civapattiyiṉi viḷikkum ciṟanta civaneṟic celvaṉākavum
tikaḻntuḷḷāṉ. icceyti tillip peruṅkōyililum tiruvatiki vīraṭṭāṉattilum ivaṉ
ceytuḷḷa tiruppaṇikaḷi veṇpā naṭiyiṟ pōṟṟi variyap peṟṟuḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷāl iṉitu
pulaṉākum. tillip peruṅkōyilukku ivaṉ ceytuḷḷa tiruppaṇikaḷik kuṟitta
muppattēḻu veṇpākkaḷ citamparam kalveṭṭiṟ poṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām.
{1} tillic ciṟṟampalattil uḷḷa tirukkoṭuṅkip pakutiyip poṉṉāl vēyntatu, (2)
tilliyiṟ poṉṉampalattic cempoṉṉāl vēyntatu, (3) tillip peruṅ kōyililuḷḷa
tiruñāṉacampantar tirukkōyilip poṉṉāl vēyntatu, (4) tivlip peruṅkōyililuḷḷa
pērampalattiṟku cepput takaṭu vēyntatu. (5) vaṭanāṭṭup pakiyaracar tiṟiyākat tanta
cempoṉṉit tillik kūtta perumāṉukkup parikalamākac ceytaḷittatu, (6)
kūttapperumāṉukkup poṟpaṭikkam ceytu koṭuttatu, {7} cem poṟkāḷañ ceytu
koṭuttatu. kūttap perumaṉukku nīṇṭeriyum kaṟpūra viḷakku amittatu. poṉṉampalattiṉic
cūḻnta tiruccuṟṟil poṉviḷakkamittatu, tillip perumāṉukku nāḷtōṟum pālamutu
nivētikka nipantam aḷittatu. āyiram nāḻi neyyāl kūttap perumāṉ tirumañcaṉañ
ceytaruḷak kaṇṭu makiḻntatu, civaperumāṉ ñāṉaṅ kuḻittaḷitta civañāṉap pālip parukiya
tiruñāṉa campantap piḷḷiyār pāṭiyaruḷiya tiruppatikam mutalākavuḷḷa tēvārat
tiruppatikaṅkaḷi nāḷun taṭiyiṉṟi ōtuvataṟkum palar kūṭiyiruntu kēṭṭu makiḻvataṟkum
uriya vaṇṇam tēvāra maṇṭapattik kaṭṭiyatu, tillip peruṅkōyilil mali pōṉṟu uyarnta
nūṟṟukkāl maṇṭapattik kaṭṭiyatu tilliyampalattic cūḻat tiruccuṟṟu māḷiki amittatu,
civakaṅkit tīrttattukkuk karuṅkaṟkaḷāl paṭiyamittatu, tillit tiruvīti cūḻa nalla oḷi
viḷakkum avvītiyiṉ nāṟṟiciyilum iṟivaṉ viṟṟirukkun tirumaṇṭapamum ceytatu,
tillik kūttapperumāṉ iṭappākattēyuḷḷa civakāmiyammikkut taṉik kōyilākac
civakāmakkōṭṭam amittatu; akkōṭṭattiṉic cūḻat tiruccuṟṟu māḷiki amittatu;
civakāmiyaṉṉikku nāḷ tōṟum tirumañcaṉañ ceytu vaḻipāṭu nikaḻtta ēṟpāṭu ceytatu,
civakāmiyaṉṉikkup poṉṉāṭi cāttiyatu, ulakam īṉṟa aṉṉiyiṉ aruḷi niṉintu pōṟṟum
muṟiyil akkōyilil nāḷtōṟum kuḻantikaṭkup pālum eṇṇeyum vaḻaṅka tipantam ceytatu,
puliyūrc ciṟṟampalattip poṉmayamāṉa koṭikaḷāl alaṅkarittatu, tilliyil kūttappirāṉ
tiruvītiyil eḻuntaruḷumpōtu naṟumpuki kamaḻa ēṟpāṭu ceytatu, attiruviḻāvil
tiruñāṉacampantar mutalāṉa civa camaya āciriyarkaḷiṉ tiruvuruvamāṉa tirukkōlaṅkaḷi oḷi
peruka eḻuntaruḷac ceytatu, tillip perumāṉi vaḻipaṭum aṉparkaḷ tilliyil
taṅkiyiruntu uṇavu koḷḷum muṟiyil, aṉṉam pālikka. aṟakkaṭṭaḷi vakuttatu, mutalām
kulōttuṅkaṉ paṭṭattaraciyāciya tiyākavalliyiṉ peyarāl nilam vāṅki annilattiṟkuriya
variyi nīkkit tilli vāḻantaṇarkku vītiyil maṉi kaṭṭik koṭuttatu, tillipperuṅ
kōyilukkuk kaḷiṟṟu yāṉi koṭuttatu, tiruñāṉacampantar, tirunāvukkaracar,
cuntaramūrtti cuvāmikaḷ āciya mūvarum ellām valla mutalvaṉākiya civaperumāṉatu pukaḻt
tiṟattip pāṭiyavāṟu ammūvar aruḷiya tēvārap patikaṅkaḷ muḻuvatiyum muṉpōṟ
citalarikkavoṇṇātavāṟu aḷavotta ceppēṭukaḷil eḻutit tillipperuṅkōyiliṟ cēmittu
vittatu, tillik kūttapperumāṉukkut tirunantavaṉam amittatu, tillip perumāṉ
mācimaka nāḷil kaṭalāṭṭiṟku eḻuntaruḷun tiruviḻāvil ammutalvaṉ vīṟṟiruntaruḷak
kiḷḷik kaṭaṟṟuṟiyil maṇṭapam amittatu, tillip peruṅkōyilukku āyiram pacukkaḷi
aḷittatu, tilliyil puli maṭuviṉ arukē viyākkirapātar tantiyār mattiyantaṉa muṉivar
vaḻipaṭṭa cuṭaliyamarntār tirukkōyilik kaṟṟaḷiyākkiyatu. tilli mūvāyiravarākiya
antaṇarkaḷ celvap perukkamuṭiyarātal vēṇṭum eṉṉum nalla nōkkattuṭaṉ tilliyarukēyuḷḷa
ēriyiliruntu taṇṇīr pāytaṟkaminta matakiṉik kalliṉāṟ ceytamittatu ākiya paṇikaḷ
mēṟkuṟitta ceyyuṭ kalveṭṭil viritturittup pārāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟē maṇaviṟ
kūttaṉ kāliṅkarāyaṉākiya ivaṉ tilliyiṟ pōṉṟē tiruvatiki tiruvīraṭṭāṉat
tirukkōyililum pala tiruppaṇikaḷic ceytuḷḷāṉ. appaṇikaḷ yāvum akkōyilil
variyappeṟṟuḷḷa irupattintu veṇpākkaḷil eḻil peṟa viritturikkap peṟṟuḷḷami
kāṇalām.
ivvāṟu cōḻaratu araciyal āṭcivaḷam peṟavum, tamiḻakattiṉ aruḷiyalāṭci muṟiyākiya
civaneṟi vaḷarcci peṟavum maṇaviṟkūttaṉ kāliṅkarāyaṉ ceytuḷḷa tiruppaṇikaḷil
ciṟappuṭiyatākak kuṟikkat takuvatu. tēlāra āciriyar mūvarum pāṭiya tiruppatikaṅkaḷ
muḻuvatiyum ceppēṭukaḷil variyac ceytu civarāca tāṉiyākat tikaḻum tillip
peruṅkōyiliṟ cēmittu vitta tiruneṟit tamiḻp paṇiyēyākum. ittiruppaṇiyi
viritturittup pōṟṟum muṟiyil amintatu.
'muttiṟattār īcaṉ mutaṟṟiṟattip pāṭiyavā(ṟu)
ottamitta ceppēṭṭi ṉuḷḷeḻuti - ittalattiṉ
ellik kirivāy iciyeḻuti ṉāṉ kūttaṉ
tillikciṟ ṟampalattē ceṉṟu'
      (te. i. ka. tokuti IV. pakkam 34, ceyyuḷ 55).
eṉa varum veṇpāvākum.
iṟivaṉ tirukkōyilukkuc ceyyum tiruppaṇi, nāṭṭil vāḻum ēḻiyeḷiya makkaḷukkum
naṟpayaṉaḷittal vēṇṭum eṉṉum perunōkkuṭiyavaṉ kāliṅkarāyaṉ eṉpatu, avaṉ tillic
civakāmiyammi tirukkōyilil nāḷ tōṟum iḷaṅkuḻantikaḷukkup pālum eṇṇeyum
vaḻaṅkumāṟu aṟakkaṭṭaḷi vakuttuḷḷa camutāyat toṇṭiṉāl naṉkuṇarappaṭum. ic ceyti.
“celvi tiruntaṟaṅkaḷ teṉṉakarit tillikkē
nallamakap pāleṇṇey nāṭōṟuñ cellattāṉ
kaṇṭāṉ arumpiyarkōṉ kaṇṇakaṉīr ñālamellāṅ
koṇṭāṉan toṇṭiyār kōṉ"
      (te. i. tokuti IV. pak. 34, pāṭal 47)
eṉavarum veṇpāvil viritturikkap peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām.
tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷiyār mutaliya tēvāra āciriyarkaḷ mūvarum pāṭiyaruḷiya
tēvārattiruppatikaṅkaḷi taṭiyiṉṟi ellārum iruntu kēṭṭaṟkuriya tēvarāya maṇṭapattit
tilliyampala muṉṟililē kāliṅkarāyaṉ kaṭṭiṉāṉ, icceyti.
naṭṭap perumāṉār ñāṉaṅ kuḻittaḷitta
ciṭṭap perumāṉ tiruppatiyam- muṭṭāmik
kēṭpōrkku maṇṭapattic ceytāṉtev vēntarkeṭa
vāṭpōkkut toṇṭiyarkōṉ maṉ'
      (te. i.ka. tokuti IV, pak. 34)
eṉavarum veṇpāliṟ kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu. tillip poṉṉampala muṉṟililē ippoḻutu
kōvintarācap perumāḷ cannitiyiṉ - kiḻakkup pakutiyē kāliṅkarāyaṉ kaṭṭiya tēvāra
maṇṭapam irunta iṭamākum. ippakutiyil tēvāra āciriyar mūvarkkum tillik
kūttapperumāṉi nōkkiya niliyil cannitiyiruntatu. iccannitiyilirunta tēvāra
āciriyar mūvar tiruvuruvamum ippoḻutu poṉṉampalattic cūḻavuḷḷa mutaṟpirakārattil
paramāṉanta kūpattiṉ etirē piravar cannitiyiyoṭṭi eḻuntaruḷac ceyyap peṟṟami kāṇalām.
vikkirama cōḻaṉukkup piṉ avaṉ mintaṉ iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ ki.pi. 1133 mutal
1150 vari in nāṭṭiṉic ciṟanta muṟiyil āṭci purinta peruvēntaṉāvāṉ. ivvēntaṉit
"tillit tirunakar ciṟappuṭittākat tirumuṭi cūṭiya kulōttuṅka cōḻa tēvar" (te.
i.ka tokuti VII eṇ 780) eṉat tirumāṇikuḻiyiluḷḷa kal veṭṭu ciṟappittup
pōṟṟukiṉṟatu. eṉavē ivaṉatu āṭcik kālattil tillip peruṅkōyil tiruppaṇikaḷ pala
iṉitu niṟivēṟṟappeṟṟut tillit tirunakaram mikavum ciṟappuṭiyatākat tikaḻvatāyiṟṟu
eṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākum. iraṇṭām kulōttuṅkaṉākiya ivvēntaṉ tillic ciṟṟampalap -
perumāṉ pāl aḷavilāp pēraṉpuṭiyavaṉ eṉpatu, "tilliyil naṭañcey kamalaṅkaḷi vaḷikkum
cinti yapayaṉ" (kulōttuṅkaṉ piḷḷittamiḻ:-78) eṉavum, "nava nititūuy, ēttaṟ
karuṅkaṭavuḷ elliyil āṉantak kūttik kaḷikūrak kumpiṭṭu” (kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉulā vari
74-76) eṉavum varum oṭṭak kūttar vāymoḻiyālum, 'tillik kūttapirāṉ tiruvaṭit
tāmariyiluḷḷa . aruḷāciya tēṉip parukum vaṇṭu pōṉṟavaṉ' (te. i. ka. tokuti IV eṇ
397) eṉat tiruvārurkkalveṭṭu ivvēntaṉatu civapattit tiṟattiṉik kuṟippiṭutalālum
naṉku viḷaṅkum.
ivvēntaṉ. tillit tirunakariṉ nāṉkuperuvītikaḷiyum vaṉappuṭiyaṉavāka aḻakupaṭuttiṉāṉ,
paṟpala maṇṭapaṅkaḷik kaṭṭuvittāṉ. civaperumāṉ tirukkūttiyaṟṟiyaruḷum
tirucciṟṟampalattit tammuṉṉōr ceytatu pōlavē poṉṉālum maṇikaḷālum aṇi peṟac
ceytāṉ. tirucciṟṟampalattiṉ mukappākiya 'etirampalattiyum' uṭkōpurattiyum,
tiruccuṟṟu māḷikiyiyum poṉmayamākkiṉāṉ. eḻunilik kōpuraṅkaḷik kaṭṭiṉāṉ. taṉ
tanti vikkirama cōḻaṉ kālattiṟ cuṭṭappeṟṟa civakāmak kōṭṭattiṉi umātēviyār tām
tōṉṟiya imayamaliyiyum maṟakkumpaṭi mēlum virivuṭiyatākkiṉāṉ. civakāmiyammiyār
tiruviḻā nāḷil ulā varutaṟkup poṉṉiṉum maṇiyiṉum aṇi ceyyap peṟṟa tēriṉic
ceytu koṭuttāṉ; poṉṉālākiya kaṟpakat tarukkaḷi amittāṉ, ammi irukkōyilukku
etirēyuḷḷa civakaṅkit tīrttattiṉic cūḻa nāṟpuṟamum maṇṭapam amittāṉ. ivvāṟu
ivvēntaṉ tillic ciṟṟampalatti virivupaṭuttum tiruppaṇikaḷic ceyyat toṭaṅkiya pōtu.
ki.pi. eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil tillit tirumuṟṟattil nantivarmaṉāl piratiṭṭi ceyyap paṭṭa
tillik kōvintarācar kōyili iṭamākap paṟṟi vāḻnta elavaṉivar cilar ittiruppaṇikkut
taṭiyāy iṭiyūṟu pala purintaṉar. atu kaṇṭu ciṉamuṟṟa ivvēntaṉ attirumāl mūrttatti
iṭam peyarttup piṉṉart tāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa tilliyampalat tiruppaṇiyiṉi iṉitu
niṟivēṟṟiṉaṉ eṉpatu varalāṟu.
ivvēntaṉ tilliyil civakāmi ammi tirukkōyilit teṉ ticimēru eṉṉumpaṭi palarum
vantu paṇintu taṅkumpaṭi uyarnta vimāṉamum maṇṭapamum uṭiyatāka virivupaṭuttiṉāṉ
eṉpataṉi,
“nīṭiya veṇṭici nīḻal vāyppa'
nēriya tekkiṇa mēru veṉṉap
pīṭiki tilli vaṉattamitta
periyaperumāḷi vāḻttiṉavē"
      (takkayākapparaṇi)
eṉa varum tāḻiciyil oṭṭakkūttar kuṟittuḷḷār. tilli yampalattic cūḻntuḷḷa iraṇṭām
tiruccuṟṟiṉik tirumāḷikip pattiyuṭaṉ amittavaṉ iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅkaḷ. tillip
poṉṉampalattic cūḻntuḷḷa iraṇṭām tiruccuṟṟu, kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ tirumāḷiki eṉa
vaḻaṅkappeṟṟatu.
aruṇmoḻit tēvarākiya cēkkiḻāraṭikaḷik koṇṭu civaṉaṭiyārkaḷ aṟupattu mūvar
varalāṟukaḷiyum kuṟittut tiruttoṇṭar purāṇamākiya varalāṟṟuk kāppiyattip pāṭumpaṭi
ceytavaṉ iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉē eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷariṟ perumpālōr tuṇipākum.
tilliyam palavaṉ 'ulakelām' eṉṟu aṭiyeṭuttuk koṭukkat tirut toṇṭar purāṇattip
pāṭiyaruḷiya cēkkiḻār nāyaṉār tammi ātarittup pōṟṟiya cōḻa maṉṉaṉip pattu iṭaṅkaḷil
pārāṭṭip pōṟṟiyuḷḷār. appāṭalkaḷuḷ anapāyaṉ eṉṟa peyariyē ciṟappākak
kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.
“ceṉṉi apayaṉ kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉ
tillittiruvelli poṉṉiṉ mayamākkiya
vaḷavar pōrēṟu eṉṟum puvikākkum
maṉṉar perumāṉ anapāyaṉ”
      (periyapurāṇam caṇṭēcar.8)
eṉavarum pāṭalil ‘tillit tiruvelli poṉṉiṉ 'mayamākkiya vaḷalar pōrēṟu' eṉac
cēkkiḻār nāyaṉār tillippatiyil ivaṉukkuḷḷa perumpaṟṟiṉip pulappaṭuttiyuḷḷami
kāṇalām.
tirunīṟṟuccōḻaṉ
tillinakar ciṟappuṭittākat tirumuṭi cūṭi iṟivaṉatu tiruvaruḷ vaṇṇamākiya tirunīṟṟiṉ
oḷivaḷarat tillip peruṅkōyiliṟ palavakit tiruppaṇikaḷiyum ārvamuṭaṉ ceytu
ittirukkōyilip poṉmayamākkiyavaṉ anapāyaṉ iṉṉum ciṟappup peyaruṭiya cōḻa maṉṉaṉātaliṉ
avvēntar perumāṉic civaneṟic celvar palarum tirunīṟṟuc cōḻaṉ eṉac ciṟappittup
pōṟṟuvārāyiṉar. tilliyampalavāṇarpāl vitta pēraṉpiṉ tiṟattāl tillip peruṅ
kōyiliṟ civattiruppaṇikaḷ purintu tilliyampalavāṇar eṭutta poṟpātattiṉ kīḻ eṉṟum
piriyātamārn tiṉpuṟum teyvanili kivarap peṟṟa ic cōḻar perumāṉi umāpati
civācāriyār tām iyaṟṟiya kōyiṟpurāṇattil,
“oṉṟiya cīr iravikulam uvantaruḷi yulakuyyat
tuṉṟu pukaḻt tirunīṟṟuc cōḻaṉeṉa muṭi cūṭi
maṉṟiṉaṭan toḻutelli vaḷarkaṉaka mayamākki
veṉṟipuṉi yanapāyaṉ viḷaṅkiya pūṅkaḻal pōṟṟi"
      (kōyil- pāyiram-12)
eḷavarum pāṭalāl vaṇaṅkip pōṟṟiyuḷḷār. tillip perumāṉ tiruvītikku eḻuntaruḷum
pōtu vināyakar, murukaṉ camayācāriyar mutaliyōr tiruvuruvaṅkaḷōṭu tirunīṟṟuc
cōḻaṉākiya ivvēntar perumāṉ tiruvuruvamum eḻuntaruḷac ceyyap peṟṟatu eṉṟa ceyti
tilliyulāvil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷami iṅkuc ciṟappākak kuṟippiṭattakuvatākum.
“tilliyil naṭañcey kamalaṅkaḷi vaḷikkuñ cinti yapyaṉ" eṉak kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ piḷḷit
tamiḻilum,
ēttaṟ karuṅkaṭavuḷ elliyi lāṉantak
kūttik kaḷikūrak kumpiṭṭu”
eṉak kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉulāvīlum kaviccakkaravartti ōṭṭakkūttar tilliyampatiyil
anapāyaṉākiya ivvēntaṉukkuḷḷa īṭupāṭṭiṉip pulappaṭuttiyuḷḷami iṅku oppu
nōkkattakulatākum. ivaṉ mintaṉ iraṇṭām irācarāca cōḻaṉatu 17-ām āṭciyāṇṭil
tirumaḻapāṭik kōyilil variyap peṟṟuḷḷa kalveṭṭoṉṟil jayaṅkoṇṭa cōḻamaṇṭalattu
......... kuṉṟattūrc cēkkiḻāṉmātēvaṭikaḷ rāmatēvaṉāṉa uttama cōḻap pallavarāyaṉ eṉpār
tirumaḻapāṭit tiruk kōyilukkut toṇṇūṟu pērāṭukaḷ utaviya ceyti
kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
'attakiya pukaḻvōraṇ marapiṟcēkki
      ḻār kuṭiyil vanta aruṇmoḻit tēvarkkut
tattupari vaḷavaṉuntaṉ ceṅkō lōccun
      talimiyaḷit tavar tamakkut taṉatu pērum
uttama cōḻap pallavaṉ tāṉeṉṉum
      uyar paṭṭaṅ koṭuttiṭa āṅkavar nīrnāṭṭu
nintaṉuṟi tirunākēc curattilaṉpu
      niṟitaliṉāl maṟavāta nilimi mikkār'
eṉac cēkkiḻār purāṇaṅ kūṟumāṟu, kuṉṟattūriṟ cēkkiḻār kuṭiyiṟ piṟantu, iraṇṭām
irācarācaṉ 17-ām āṭci yāṇṭil kōyilukku nīyattamaḷitta kuṉṟattūrc cēkkiḻāṉ
mātēvaṭikaḷ rāmatēvaṉāṉa uttamacōmappallavarāyaṉ eṉpavarē cēkkiḻār nāyaṉār eṉpatum, ivari
ātarittut tiruttoṇṭar purāṇattip pāṭacceyta anapāyaṉ eṉpavaṉ iraṇṭām irācarācaṉuṭiya
tanti iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ kāṉpatum, cēkkiḻāruṭiya tampiyār pālaṟāvāyar eṉpavar
iraṇṭām irācarācaṉatu 19-ām āṭciyāṇṭil tiruvaṟattuṟiyil nikaḻum māci vikāci
viḻākkaḷil tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷiyār māṟaṉpāṭikku eḻuntaruḷutaṟku
nilamaḷittakuṉṟattūrc cēkkiḻāṉ pālaṟāvāyaṉ - kaḷappāḷarāyaṉ eṉpatu, ivar
iraṇṭāṅkulōttuṅkaṉatu āṭciyiṉ iṭippakuti toṭaṅki mūṉṟāṅkulōttuṅkaṉ āṭciyiṉ
muṟpakutivari vāḻntavareṉpatum aṟiñar mu. irākaviyaṅkār mutaliya ārāycciyāḷar
tuṇipākum.
tilli nakarcciṟappuṭittākat tirumuṭi cūṭiya iraṇṭām kulōttuṅkacōḻaṉ
tillipperuṅkōyilil eḻunilik kōpuraṅkaḷik kaṭṭiṉāṉ. cēkkiḻāraṭikaḷ periyapurāṇam
pāṭutaṟku muṉṉēyē tillip peruṅkōyililuḷḷa eḻu nilik kōpuraṅkaḷ nāṉkum
kaṭṭappeṟṟiruntaṉa eṉpatu,
'nīṭuvāṉpaṇiya uyarnta poṉvaripōl niliyeḻukōpuram'
      (periya-taṭut-109)
eṉavum,
"niliyēḻ kōpuram muṟiyē koṭutoḻu tuṉpukkār” (௸.tirunāvuk-194)
eṉavum,
“nīṭu nīṇilik kōpurattuḷ pukku"       (௸. tiruñāṉa -158)
eṉavum varum cēkkiḻār vāymoḻiyāl naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
tilliyiṉ mēṟkuk kōpuram cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ kōpuram eṉak kalveṭṭukkaḷiṟ
kuṟikkappeṟiṉum (te.i.ka tokuti IV eṇ 628-30) atu avaṉukku muṉ aracāṇṭa
cōḻarkaḷālē kaṭṭappeṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum eṉavum, ataṉi mēlum putuppittavaṉ mutal
caṭiyavarmaṉ cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ eṉavum avvāṟē tilliyiṉ teṟkukkōpurattip putuppittuk
cokkacīyaṉ eṉṟu peyariṭṭavaṉ mutaṟkōpperuñciṅkaṉ eṉavum, vaṭakkukkōpurattip
patuppittavar kiruṣṇa tēvarāyar eṉavum koḷḷa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. innāṉku kōpuraṅkaḷiṉ
amippum ivaṟṟiṉ mēluḷḷa karuṅkaṟciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ vaṭiva amippum ciṟpattiṉi vaṭinta
kaṟkaḷum orē taṉmiyavāka amintiruttalik kūrntu nōkkuṅkāl in nāṉku kōpuraṅkaḷum
cōḻa maṉṉarkaḷāl amikkappeṟṟuc cēkkiḻār kālattilēyē ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉa eṉpatu iṉitu
pulaṉākum.
"cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉ tirunili yeḻu kōpuraccaṉṉatiyil cokkaccīyaṉ kuṟaḷil, kīḻppakkattuk
kīḻimaṭastāṉamākat tirunōkkaḻakiyāṉ tirumaṭameṉṉum pērāl ceyvitta maṭattukku
maṭacēṣamāka nāyakarum nācciyārum eḻuntaruḷum nāḷ ... etirilicōḻaṉ
tirunantavaṉattukku.” (te. i: ka , to. IV eṇ 624) - eṉavum "srīkiruṣṇatēvamakārāyaṉ
taṉmamāka simhāttiṟi poṭṭuṉūṟku eḻuntaruḷi jayastampam nāṭṭi tirumpi
poṉṉampalattukku eḻuntaruḷi poṉṉampala nātaṉiyum cēvittu, vaṭakkuk kōpuram kaṭṭi
vittacēvi" (௸ eṇ 623)
eṉavum varum kalveṭṭukkaḷ cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ kaṭṭiyatu mēlikkōpuram eṉpataṉiyum vaṭakkuk
kōpuram kiruṣṇatēvarāyarāl kaṭṭappeṟṟa-teṉpataṉiyum kuṟittuḷḷami kāṇalām.
"irājākkaḷ tampirāṉ tirumāḷiki mēlittirumāḷikiyil niliyeḻukōparat tiruvācal
puṟavācal teṉ pakkattu eḻuntaruḷiyiruntu pūcikoṇṭaruḷukiṟa kulōttuṅkacōḻa
vināyakar" eṉappāṇṭiyar kalveṭṭiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa tala vināyakar mēlikkōpurattuṭaṉ
iṇintuḷḷa tirumēṉiyātalāl apperumāṉ eḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷa mēlikkōpuram kulōttuṅka
cōḻaṉāl mutaṉ mutaṟ kaṭṭappeṟṟateṉpatu uyattuṇarappaṭum.
ki.pi.1178 mutal 1218 vari tamiḻakatti āṭci purintacōḻa maṉṉaṉ mūṉṟāṅkulōttuṅkaṉ
āvaṉ. ivaṉ tilliyil ciṟṟampalattiṟku muṉṉuḷḷatum ippoḻutu kaṉakacapiyeṉa vaḻaṅkap
peṟuvatum ākiya etirampalatṉatap poṉṉiṉāl vēyntāṉ. kūttapperumāṉukkuc
cittirittiṅkaḷil tiruviḻā nikaḻa ēṟpāṭu ceytāṉ. civakāmiyammi tirukkōyiliṉ
vimāṉamākiya kōpurattip poṉṉāl vēyntāṉ. ivaṉ tilliyiṟceyta ittiruppaṇikaḷ
"ettariyum toḻum iṟivaṟku etirampalam cempoṉ vēyntu, cittiri viḻā amittu
iṟivitirukkōpuram cempoṉ vēyntu" eṉavarum ivaṉuṭiya meykkīrttiyiṟ
kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām. iṅku etirampalam eṉṟatu cēkkiḻār kūṟum pērampalamē
eṉavum, etirampalam poṉ vēynta mūṉṟāṅkulōttuṅkaṉē tiruttoṇṭar purāṇam kūṟum
pērampalam poṉ vēynta anapāyaṉ eṉavum, cēkkiḻāri ātarittut tirut toṇṭar purāṇam
pāṭu vittavaṉ immūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉē eṉavum kūṟuvar ārāycciyaṟiñar
catācivapaṇṭārattār.
"capāpatiyiṉ muṉṉuḷḷa, mukamaṇṭapattiyum malimakaḷ (civakāmiyammi) kōyiliṉ
kōpurattiyum cuṟṟiyuḷḷa pirakāramāḷikikaḷiyum avvūrp perumāṉiṭattē iṭiyaṟāta
pakti koṇṭa ivvaracaṉ poṉmayamāka viḷaṅkum paṭi nirmāṇittāṉ." eṉat tiripuvaṉa
vīrēccurat tirukkōyilil variyappaṭṭuḷḷa vaṭamoḻikkalveṭṭum, ivvēntaṉ tilliyiṟ ceyta
tiruppaṇikaḷik kūṟiyuḷmmami kāṇalām. ikkalveṭṭil mukamaṇṭapam eṉṟatu,
tillicciṟṟampalavar tirumuṉ koṭi marattiṉ teṉ ticiyil amintuḷḷa niruttacapiyeṉak
koḷḷutal ēṟpuṭiyatākum. ic capiyāṉatu kutirikaḷ pūṭṭiya tēriṉ amippiṉiyuṭiya
tāyiruttaliṉ itu tēr maṇṭapam eṉavum vaḻaṅkap peṟum. (caṅkaṟpa nirākaraṇam),
immaṇṭapattiṉ tūṇkaḷ yāvum ivvēntaṉāl tañci māvaṭṭam tiripuvaṉattil niṟuvap peṟṟa
tiripuvaṉa vīrēccurattiluḷḷa tūṇkaḷiṉ amippiṉiyuṭiyaṉavāka iruttalālum ivaṉatu
vaḻipaṭu mūrtta mākat tiripuvaṉattiluḷḷa carapamūrttiyiṉ tirumēṉi iccapiyilum
iruttalālum immaṇṭapattiṉ vaṭapuṟattu aṭip paṭṭiyalil kūttapperumāṉi nōkkik
kumpiṭum niliyil ivaṉatu uruvap paṭivam nērē amikkap peṟṟiruttalālum naṉku
viḷaṅkum. ciṟiya aḷavil iccapiyil uḷḷa ivvēntaṉatu karuṅkaṟ paṭivamum tiripuvaṉa
vīrēccura vimāṉattil uḷḷa ivaṉatu cutippaṭivamum oṉṟāyiruttalāl in niruttacapiyik
kaṭṭiyavaṉ tiripu vīrēccurat tirukkōyili niṟuviya mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉē eṉpatu
naṉku teḷiyappaṭum.
tilliyil tirumāḷikip pattiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya mūṉṟām pirākāram irājākkaḷ tampirāṉ
eṉṉum ciṟappup peyariṉiyuṭiya mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉāl amikkap peṟṟatākum.
atupaṟṟiyē itu irācākkaḷ tampirāṉ tirumāḷikiyeṉa kalveṭṭil vaḻaṅkap peṟukiṉṟatu.
cērapāṇṭiya maṇṭalamākiya pāṇṭi nāṭṭi ivvēntaṉ kippaṟṟiya niliyil annāṭṭil
tiṟiyākap peṟṟa pacumpoṉṉum viḷi nilaṅkaḷum ākiyavaṟṟip perumpaṟṟappuliyūr
tilliyampalattilē āṭal puriyum kūttap perumāṉuṭaṉ iruntu am mutalvaṉatu aruḷ
naṭaṉaṅkaṇṭaruḷum cilakāmiyammikkum tiruvārūrpperumāṉukkum tiripuvaṉa vīrēccurattu
iṟivaṉukkum maturittiruvālavāy iṟivaṉukkum koṭuttu makiḻntāṉ. icceyti,
'ciṟi koṇṭa puṉal viyic cēra pāṇṭiya maṇṭalattu
iṟi koṇṭa pakam poṉṉum iṟiyiliyu meyiṟpuliyūr
āṭum ampalavāṇar kūṭi vāynta tirunaṭaṅ kaṇṭaruḷum
pāṭakakkāṟ piṅkiḷikkum pimpoṉ matiḷ tiruvārūr
vāṉavaṟkum tiripuvaṉa vīrīccura aruntavaṟkum
tēṉviricaṭit tiruvālavāyc ceḻuñcuṭarkkuṅ
koṭuttaruḷi'
eṉavarum ivvēntaṉatu yeyccīrttiyāl aṟiyappaṭum.
ivvēntaṉatu muppattāṟāvatu āṭciyāṇṭil tillic ciṟṟampalattil tiru aṇukkaṉ
tiruvāyili oṭṭi, aminta kaṉaka capiyākiya etirampalattiṉ aṭippīṭamākiya kuṟaṭu
ivvēntaṉāl poṟṟakaṭu pōrttap peṟṟatu. poṟṟakaṭu pōrttuvataṟkumuṉ akkuṟaṭṭil variyap
peṟṟirunta kalveṭṭu paṭiyeṭukkap peṟṟu iraṇṭām pirakārattiṉiyoṭṭiya vāyilākiya
kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ tirumāḷikip puṟavāyil vaṭapakkattiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭatu. mutaṟ
kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉatu nāṟpattāṟāvatu āṇṭil avvēntaṉuṭiya taṅkiyār maturāntaki āḻvār
tirucciṟṟampala muṭiyārkkut tirunantavaṉamākavum; srī mākēsvararkkut tiruvamutu ceyya
maṭappuṟamākavum nilamaḷitta ceytiyiṉik kūṟuvatu ikkalveṭṭākum.
tillip perukkōyiliṉ mūṉṟām pirākāratti irācākkaḷ tampirāṉ tirumāḷiki eṉat taṉ
peyarāl amitta mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ attiruccuṟṟiṉ mēlip pirākārattil taṉ
muṉṉōr niṟuviya cilakāmiyammi tiruk kōyilukkut tiruppaṇi ceytatōṭu
ataṉiyaṭuttuḷḷa murukapperumāṉ tirukkōyilākiya pāṇṭiya nāyakat tirukkōyiliyum
niruttacapiyiṉip pōṉṟu periya tērmaṇṭapamāka amittuḷḷāṉ eṉak karuta vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.
tillikkōyilil aṇṭamuṟa nimirntāṭum kūttap perumāṉukku amikkappeṟtuḷḷa
niruttacapiyilum aṟumukapperumāṉ vaḷḷi teyvayāṉiyuṭaṉ amarntaruḷiya
pāṇṭiyanāyakattilum amintuḷḷa tūṇkaḷ mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉāl niṟuvappeṟṟuḷḷa
tiripuvaṉa vīrēccurattil uḷḷa tuṇkaḷi yotta amippuṭiyaṉavākavum, im maṇṭapaṅkaḷiluḷḷa
ciṟpa aṉamappukaḷ oru kālattaṉavākavum kāṇappaṭuvatālum ili tamiḻakatti nāṟpatu āṇṭukaḷ
nalampeṟa āṭcipurinta mūṉnāṅkulōttuṅkaṉāl amikkap peṟṟaṉavē eṉak karuta
vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. ivvēntaṉ pāṇṭiyarkaḷi veṉṟu pāṇṭi maṇṭalattiṟkuc cōḻa pāṇṭiya maṇṭalam
eṉavum, maturimānakarkku muṭittali koṇṭa cōḻapuram eṉavum, maturiyaraṇmaṉiyiluḷḷa
kolumaṇṭapattiṟkuc cēra pāṇṭiyar tampirāṉ eṉavum taṉ peyarkaḷi vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷāṉ avvāṟē
ittillip patiyil pāṇṭiyartam pirāṉākiya mūṉṟāṅ kuvōttuṅkaṉāṟkaṭṭap peṟṟa murukaṉ
kōyilum pāṇṭiya nāyakam eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟuvatāyiṟṟu eṉatterikiṟatu. tillit
tirukkōyiliṟ cēkkiḻār perumāṉ amarntu tiruttoṇṭar purāṇatti iyaṟṟutaṟkum,
urivirittaṟkum iṭamāka viḷaṅkiyatu āyirakkāl maṇṭapamākum. itu mūṉṟāṅ
kulōttuṅkaṉākiya, ivvēntaṉāṟ kaṭṭap peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. eṉpatu ārāycciyaṟiñar
catāciva paṇṭārattār tuṇipākum.
mutal kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉatu nāṟpattāṟām āṭciyāṇṭil miḻalināṭṭu vēḷāṇmi koṇṭa kaṇṭaṉ
mātavaṉ eṉpāṉ tilliyampalattiṉ vaṭakīḻtticiyil purāṇa nūl virikkum
puricimaḷikiyiṉi amittāṉ eṉṟa ceyti muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭatu, appurāṇamaṇṭapattiṉi
āyirkkāl maṇṭapamāka virivupaṭuttiyavaṉ mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ eṉakkarututal
poruttamuṭiyatākum. āyirak kāl maṇṭapam kaṭṭum vaḻakkam ivaṉ kālattil, tāṉ
tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpar ārāycciyāḷar. tiruvārūriluḷḷa āyirakkāl maṇṭapam
tēvāciriyaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkappeṟukiṉṟatu. im maṇṭapam mūṉṟām kulōttuṅkaṉāl
kaṭṭappaṭṭateṉpatu, tiripuvaṉa vīrēccurattiluḷḷa vaṭamoḻikkalveṭṭāl aṟiyappaṭum.
ivvēntaṉatu paṭit talivaṉākiya kaṇṭar cūriyaṉ campuvarāyaṉ eṉpāṉ tiruvakkariyiluḷḷa
civaṉ kōyilil cūriyaṉ tirukkōpuramum kaṇṭar cūriyaṉ eṉṟa āyirakkāl maṇṭapamum
ākiyavaṟṟik kaṭṭiyuḷḷāṉ. eṉavē tilliyiluḷḷa āyirakkāl maṇṭapamum ivvēntaṉ
kālattilēyē kaṭṭappeṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. ivvāyirakkāl maṇṭapattilēyē cēkkiḻār
nāyaṉār tāmpāṭiya tiruttoṇṭar purāṇattiṟku uri virittaruḷiṉār eṉpatu umāpati
civācāriyār iyaṟṟiya cēkkiḻār purāṇattāl aṟiyappaṭum.
-------------
2. pāṇṭiyar
ki.pi. 1251-71 muṭicūṭiya mutal caṭiyavarmaṉ cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpāṉ palanāṭukaḷi
veṉṟamiyāl makārā cāti rācaparamēcuvaraṉ, em maṇṭalamuṅ koṇṭaruḷiyavaṉ ellān taliyāṉa
perumāḷ eṉap pala paṭṭaṅkaḷip puṉintu koṇṭāṉ. ilvēntar perumāṉ civarkaḷāl kōyil
eṉap pōṟṟappeṟum tillip peruṅ kōyilukkum, viṇavarkaḷāl kōyil eṉappōṟṟappeṟum
tiruvaraṅkam periya kōyilukkum palatiruppaṇikaḷip purintuḷḷāṉ. ivaṉ tilliyiṟ
kūttapperumāṉi vaṇaṅkip pala tulāpāratāṉaṅkaḷ ceytāṉ. tilliyampalattip poṉ vēyntāṉ.
tillit tiruk kōyiliṉ mēlik kōpuram 'cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉ tirunili eḻukōpuram'
eṉṉum peyarāl (te. i. cu. tokuti 4-6.24} kalveṭṭukkaḷiṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
eṉavē ikkōpurattip paḻutu pārttup patukkiyavaṉ ippāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ eṉpatu naṉku
pulaṉākum. ivaṉ tāṉ tulāpāram pukka poṉṉik koṇṭu, tillik kōyilukkup poṉ
vēyntuḷḷāṉ.
itu paṟṟiyē ivvēntaṉ "kōyil poṉvēynta perumāḷ" eṉap pōṟṟap peṟṟaṉaṉ. icceyti,
"vāḻka kōyil poṉ vēynta makipati
vāḻka centamiḻ māli terintavaṉ
vāḻka maṇṭalam yāviyum koṇṭavaṉ
vāḻka cuntara maṉṉavaṉ teṉṉaṉē"
      (tokuti IV 455)
eṉat tiruppuṭkuḻi tirumāl kōyilil variyappeṟṟuḷḷa kalveṭṭup pāṭalāṟ pulaṉākum.
ippāṭaliṟ "kōyil" eṉṟatu tillip peruṅ kōyiliyum, tiruvaraṅkam periya
kōyiliyum. iṅṅaṉam tilliyilum tiruvaraṅkattilumuḷḷa tirukkōyilkaḷip poṉ vēyntami
paṟṟik “kōyil poṉ vēynta perumāḷ” eṉa ivaṉ pōṟṟap peṟṟaṉaṉ eṉat terikiṟatu.
(kalveṭṭu, āṇṭaṟikki 1936-37)
ciṉavarik kimpuri veṇpiṟik kōṭṭikal veṅkaṭuṅkaṭ
ciṉamata veṅkaric cuntarat teṉṉavaṉ tilli maṉṟil
vaṉacat tiruvuṭaṉ ceñcoṟṟiruvi maṇantatākkum
kaṉakattuliyuṭaṉ muttuttuliyiṟ kalantatuvē.
      (tokuti IV 620)
eṉavarum pāṭal ivaṉ tilliyil tulāpāram pukka ceytiyi yuṇarttum. ivaṉatu patiṉōrām
āṭciyāṇṭil nantavaṉattukkuriya vari nīkkappaṭṭatu. cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ tiruttōppu, cuntara
pāṇṭiyaṉ teṟkut tiruvīti ivaṟṟiṉ vaḷarccikkākavum kuṭikaḷ vāḻvukkākavum nilam
aḷittuḷḷāṉ. (S. 1. 1. 546-18) tirumūlaṭṭāṉamuṭiyārkkut tiruvuruttiram
attiyayaṉam paṇṇa nilam aḷittuḷḷāṉ (A.R. Vol. VI 631). ivaṉatu āṭcik kālattil
1253 mutal 1268 vari cōḻanāṭu naṭunāṭu toṇṭi nāṭukaḷukku aracap
piratinitiyāyiruntu āṇṭavaṉ caṭiyavarmaṉ vīrapāṇṭiyaṉ āvāṉ. ivaṉ pallava aracaṉākiya
iraṇṭāṅ kōpperuñ ciṅkaṉiṭam tiṟi koṇṭu tilliyil - vīrāpiṭēkamum vicayāpiṭēkamum
ceytu koṇṭāṉ. icceyti
"tiṅkaḷ aravamuñ ceḻumalart tāruṭaṉ
poṅkupuṉaṟ ceñcaṭiyōṉ poṟpuliyūr vīṟṟiruntu
kāṭavaṉ tiṟiyiṭak kaṇṭiṉitiruntu
vīrāpiṣēkamum vicayāpiṣēkamum paṇṇiyaruḷiya"
srī vīrapāṇṭiya tēvarkku yāṇṭu '(putukkōṭṭik kalveṭṭut tokuti 370-372)' eṉa
varum ivaṉatu meyk kīrttit toṭarāl iṉitu pulaṉākum.
----------------
3. pallavar
cimmavarmaṉ mutaliya muṟkālap pallavarkaḷiṉ kalveṭṭukkaḷ tilliyiṟ kiṭikkavilli.
cōḻamaṉṉarkaḷukkup paṭittalivarkaḷāyiruntu piṉṉart taṉiyaracu naṭattiya piṟkālap
pallavarkaḷiṉ tiruppaṇikaḷē ikkōyiṟ kalveṭṭukkaḷil kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
kāṭavarāya maṉṉarkaḷil mikavum ciṟappuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiyavarkaḷ cāṭum perumāṉāṉa mutalāvatu
kōpperuñciṅkaṉum avaṉ makaṉ vāḷvalla perumāḷāṉa iraṇṭāvatu kōp peruñciṅkaṉum āvar.
iruvarum cakalapuvaṉac cakkaravartti, avaṉiyāḷap piṟantāṉ eṉṟa paṭṭaṅkaḷip peṟṟiruntaṉar.
mutalāvatu kōpperuñciṅkaṉ cōḻar kula iḷavaraciyit tirumaṇam purintavaṉ. ataṉāl maṇavāḷap
perumāḷ eṉap pōṟṟap peṟṟāṉ.
mutalāvatu kōpperuñciṅkaṉ taṉ āṭciyiṉ mūṉṟāvatu, nāṉkāvatu āṇṭukaḷil tillik
kōyiliṉ pāl ney tēvikaḷukkāka āyirattu eṇpattāṟu pacukkaḷi riṣapa, cūla
muttiriyuṭaṉ aḷittāṉ (S. I. I.V.ol VII.No 54}. itu ivaṉ makaṉiṉ mūṉṟām āṇṭuk
kalveṭṭilum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ki.pi. 1237-il ivaṉ tillik kōyiliṉ teṟkuk
kōpuram kaṭṭuvataṟkākat toṇṭi maṇṭalattu āṟṟūril muntāṟṟu oṉṟē mukkāl vēli nila
varuvāyiyum piṟavarikaḷ cilavaṟṟiliruntu kiṭikkum poruḷiyum, aḷittuḷḷāṉ. (S.
I. I, Vol No. 51). icceytiyit tillik kōyililum, āṟṟūr muttīsvarar
kōyililum, kāñci ēkampamuṭiyār kōyililum kallil cetukki vittāṉ. ivaṉāl
kaṭṭappaṭṭa teṟkuk kōpuram, "cokkaccīyaṉ tirunili eḻukōpurat tiruvācal" eṉṟa
peyaruṭaṉ viḷaṅkiyatu. cokkaccīyaṉ eṉpatu mutalāvatu kōpperuñ ciṅkaṉuṭiya virutup
peyarākum. ivaṉ taṉatu 15-āvatu āṭciyāṇṭil tillik kōyilukkāka vēcālip pāṭip
paṟṟup pūvāliyil 'cokkac cīyaṉ kamuku tirunantavaṉam' eṉat taṉ peyarāl oru nantavaṉam
amittāṉ, (S. I. I.. Vol. VIII No. 55). ivvaṟakkaṭṭaḷiyi ivaṉ makaṉuṭiya mūṉṟāvatu
āṇṭuk kalveṭṭu oṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatum kavaṉikkattakkatu (S.I. I. Vol. VIII. No.
53)
vāḷvalla perumāḷāṉa iraṇṭāvatu kōpperuñ ciṅkaṉ tillik kōyilil aḷavilā
īṭupāṭuṭiyavaṉ. ivaṉuṭiya āṟṟūrk kavveṭṭu ivaṉik 'kaṉaka capāpati capā carvakārya
carvakāla nirvākaṉ' (S, I, I. Vol, XII -No. 120) eṉṟum, tiripurāntakak kalveṭṭu
'kaṉakacapāpati nāta caraṇāravinta matukaramāṉavaṉ' (S. I, 1. Vol. XII, No 247) eṉṟum
kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṉatu mūṉṟāvatu āṭciyāṇṭil citamparattiṉ mēl piṭākiyāṉa vikkirama
ciṅkapurattu mēṟpuṟattil tillik kōyiliṉ tēvikkāṉa paḻaṅkaḷ kāykaḷ mutaliyavikaḷip
peṟa 'āḷiyār tiruttōppu' eṉṉum tiruttōppu amikkappaṭṭatu. (S. 1. I. Vol.
VII. No. 53). atil paṇipurivōrukkuc cokkac cīyaṉ tirunantavaṉak kuṭikaḷukku
aḷikkappaṭṭa ūtiyattip pōṉṟu aḷikka ikkāṭavaṉ ēṟpāṭu ceytāṉ. atēyāṇṭil ivaṉ
tillik kōyilukku nāṉūṟṟuppattup pacukkaḷi viṭṭāṉ. (S 1. 1. Vol. VIII, No.
54) itik kūṟum kalveṭṭu ivaṉ tanti viṭṭa pacukkaḷiyum kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. ki. pi.
1251-il ivaṉ kaṭavāccēri eṉṟu taṟpoḻutu aḻikkappaṭum tilli nāyaka nallūril cāliyar
(necavāḷar) cilarik kuṭiyamartti avarkaḷ tirukkāmak kōṭṭamuṭiya nācciyārkkuc
cāttum paricaṭṭatti aḷikka ēṟpāṭu ceytatuṭaṉ tilli nāyaka nallūrukkut 'tiruvampalap
perumāḷ puram' eṉṟa putiya peyariyum cūṭṭiṉāṉ. (S.II. Vol. XII. No. 154).
aṭutta āṇṭil ivaṉ tillik kōyilukkuriya mūṉṟu tirunantavaṉaṅkaḷiṉ parāmarippiṟkākac
cila nilaṅkaḷi aḷittāṉ. (ibid No. 154). ivaṉ kālattiltāṉ tillik kāḷi kōyil
cuṟṟaḷiyāka eṭukkappaṭṭatu. ivaṉatu 8-ām āṇṭuk kalveṭṭu akkōyil tiruppaṇi
naṭipeṟṟu vantatit teḷivākak kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu. (SI.1 Vol. VIII. No. 717).
ivaṉatu pattāvatu āṭciyāṇṭil attiruppaṇi muṭivuṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. (S. I. I,
Vol, XII No. 159). itaṟku muṉṉart tillikkāḷi 'tillivaṉamuṭiya paramēsvari'
eṉavum, piṉṉar piṭāriyār 'tirucciṟṟampala mākāḷi' eṉavum aḻikkap paṭṭatik
kalveṭṭukkaḷiliruntu aṟikiṉṟōm. ikkāḷi kōyil taṟpoḻutu paḷḷip paṭi
eṉṟaḻikkappaṭum vikkirama cōḻa nallūriṉ ellik kuṭpaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. tillik kōyiliṉ
kiḻakkukkōpuram ivaṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭati ivaṉuṭiya tiripurāntakak kalveṭṭiliruntu
aṟikiṟōm. (S. I. I. Vol. XII No. 247). nāṉku tikkilum mirunta vēntarkaḷi
veṉṟu, avar celvattik kavarntu, tulāpāram naṭatti, appoṉṉāl mērumaliyip pōṉṟa
kiḻakkuk kōpurattit taṉ peyarāl ikkāṭavaṉ eṭuppittāṉ. ikkōpurattiṉ nāṉku
pakkaṅkaḷiyum ivaṉ oḷimayamāka nirmāṇittuk kumpāpiṣēkattic ceytāṉ, ittiruppaṇi
ki.pi. 1262-ām āṇṭiṟkuḷ naṭipeṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. mēṟ kūṟiya tiripurāntakak
kalveṭṭil teṟkuk kōpurattippaṟṟiya kuṟippu etuvum illātatu kavaṉikkat takkatu. orē
peyaril viḷaṅkiya tantiyum makaṉum muṟiyē teṟkuk kōpurattiyum kiḻakkuk
kōpurattiyum kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ eṉṟa uṇmiyi maṟuppataṟkilli. iruvarum tillik
kōyilukkum, tirukkaḷāñ ceṭiyuṭiyār kōyilukkum taṉippaṭṭōr palar aḷitta
nilaṅkaḷukku varivilakkuc ceytuḷḷārkaḷ.
mutalāvatu kōpperuñ ciṅkaṉum avaṉ makaṉum nāṭṭiyak kaliyil mikunta īṭupāṭu
koṇṭavarkaḷ. iruvarum 'paratam valla perumāḷ' eṉṟa virutup peyarik koṇṭiruntaṉar.
(S.I.l. Vol. VIII. No. 69). iraṇṭāvatu kōpperuñciṅkaṉi avaṉuṭiya āṟṟūrk
kalveṭṭu 'paratamallaṉ' eṉṟum (S, 1, 1, Vol, VII. No. 120}, tiripurāntakak
kalveṭṭu 'cākitya ratṉākaraṉ' {ibid No. 247), eṉṟum aḻikkiṉṟaṉa. tillik kōyiliṉ
teṟku kiḻakkuk kōpuraṅkaḷiṉ tiruvācal uṭpuṟac cuvaril paratanāṭṭiya muttirikaḷ
cetukkappaṭṭiruppatu mēṟkūṟiya kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟiṉi uṟutip paṭuttukiṉṟatu.
--------------
4. cēramaṉṉar :
cakam 1498 (ki. pi. 1576)-il cēramāṉ perumāḷ kuṭiyil tōṉṟiya kocci irāmavarma
makārājā āṉanta tāṇṭava mākēcurarkaḷukkum, antaṇarkaḷukkum, paricārakarkaḷukkum
nāḷtōṟum 33 taḷikiyaḷikka nipantam aḷittuḷḷār.
--------------
5. vicayanakara maṉṉar
makātēvarāyar II
ivar ki.pi. 1428-il tēvattāṉa nirvāka ūḻalkaḷit tiruttiṉār. atikavari
vacūlippiṉāl kuṭikaḷ kirāmaṅkaḷi viṭṭu ōṭippōyiṉar. ataṉāl varumāṉam kuṟintamiyāl
citamparam kōyil pūci taṭippaṭṭatu. ōṭip pōṉa kuṭikaḷi mīṇṭum aḻittuk kirāmaṅkaḷi
vaḷappaṭuttik kōyil nirvākattic ceppañ ceytār. {376-1913).
timmarāyar:
vīrappiratāpa timmarāyarākiya ivar cakam 1425 (ki.pi. 1503} il cuvāmi, ampāḷukku
iraṭṭi māli cāttap perumpaṟṟap puliyūrkku mēṟkēyuḷḷa kārikuṭi kirāmatti
aḷittuḷḷār.
kiruṣṇatēvarāyar
cakam 1432 (ki.pi. 1510) il aḻakiya ciṟṟampala muṭiyārkku makāpūci nikaḻavum
aṭiyārkaḷukku amutu vaḻaṅkavum ēṟpāṭu ceytār. cakam 1433 (ki.pi. 1511) il
citampara nātapuram eṉṟa kirāmattik kōyilukkuc carvamāṉiyamāka aḷittār. cakam 1431
(ki. pi. 1509) il cimmahāttiri poṭṭaṉūrukkuc ceṉṟu veṉṟu vaṭakkuk kōpurattik
kaṭṭiṉār. (S. 1, 1 of 13) (S.1.1, Vol. IV 622).
accutatēvarāyar:
ivar tirumāl mūrttatti mīṇṭum tillik kōyilil piratiṭṭi ceytu vikāṉaca vitippaṭi
pūci nikaḻa 5000 poṉ koṭuttuḷḷār.
civa, viṇava vēṟṟumiyillā ivar. cakam 1451 (ki.pi 1529)-il kūttap perumāṉ
tērviḻāviṟkāka 64, kirāmaṅkaḷiyum, vaṭakkuk kōpurat tiruppaṇikkāka 38
kirāmaṅkaḷiyum carva māṉiyamākak koṭuttuḷḷār. (Oriental Research Vol. 12. P.
169-178).
vēṅkaṭa tēvarāyar
cakam 1500 (ki.pi. 1578) civakāma cuntariyammikkut tiruvaṉantal pūcikkākak
kirāmaṅkaḷi aḷittār. cakam 1506 {ci.pi. 1613}-il 300 poṉ koṭuttu ēḻikaḷukku
nāḷtōṟum 20 kaṭṭic cōṟu aḷikkumpaṭi ēṟpāṭu ceytār. 5000 calam nel varuvāyuḷḷa
kirāmattiyaḷittu nāḷ tōṟum tuṟavikaḷukku 30 kaṭṭic cōṟu aḷikka ēṟpāṭu ceytār,
(S. 1. 1. 346, 347-13) cakam 1586 {ki.pi. 1664)- il citamparattil irunta
namacivāya uṭiyār mēṟpārviyil irupatu kaṭṭic cōṟu koṭukka nipantam aḷittuḷḷār.
iṅkuk kuṟitta namacivāya uṭiyār eṉpavar kurunamacivāyarāka iruttal kūṭumō eṉṟu
aiyuṟuvar cilar. ivaruṭiya kalveṭṭukkaḷ citamparam kōyilil 12-uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ
perumpālaṉa aṉṉam pālippup paṟṟiyaṉa.
srīraṅkarāyar II
ivar cakama 1503 (ki.pi. 1581-il miḷakut taraku varumāṉaṅ koṇṭu ciṟṟampalavarkku
apiṣēkam ceyyavum, cakam 1517 (ki.pi. 1595) il civakāmacuntari aippaci pūraviḻāk
koṇṭaruḷavum puṟappēṭṭi ūrit tēvatcaṉamākak koṭuttuḷḷār. cakam 1503 (ki.pi.
1581) - il ēḻu kirāmaṅkaḷiṉ varumāṉattik koṇṭum, miḷakuttarakik koṇṭum
perumāḷukku apiṣēka ārātaṉi viḻāc celavukaḷ naṭattivara ēṟpāṭu ceytuḷḷār.
srīraṅkarāyar VI:
ivar cakam 1565 il tillit tiruccittira kūṭattil kōvintarācap perumāḷ muṉ
maṇṭapattip paḻutu pārttār. puṇṭarīkavallit tāyār, cūṭikkoṭutta nācciyār
vimāṉaṅkaḷum paḻutupārkkap peṟṟaṉa. ivarkālattil āṭūr, karuṅkuḻi, kuriyāmaṅkalam,
maturāntaka nallūr, uṭiyūr mutaliya kirāmaṅkaḷukkut tīrvi taḷḷupaṭi ceyyappaṭṭatu. itu
nikaḻnta kālam 2.2.1644 eṉṟu el.ṭi. cāmik kaṇṇup piḷḷiyavarkaḷ kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷār.
vīrapūpatirāyar:
ivar niruttanātaṉ (kūttappirāṉ) tirumuṉ viḷakkukkāka taṉatu araciyal piratāṉi
ceṇṭapparācā ātitta rājāvik koṇṭu 64 pacukkaḷ aḷittuḷḷār. ivar kālam pukkā II
kālamāka irukkalām eṉṟu epikirāpikā (1909 pak 115) āṇṭaṟikki kūṟum
-------------
6. nāyakka maṉṉarkaḷ:
nākamanāyakkar:
maturiyil nāyakkar āṭciyi niṟuviya vicuvanāta nāyakkar tanti nākama nāyakkar
citamparattil oru carakkaṟiyi vilikku vāṅkik kōyilukku aḷittuḷḷār.
muttukkiruṣṇappa nāyakkar:-
ivar (citamparattil kōvittarācap perumāḷi mīḷavum piratiṭci ceyta) viyappa kiruṣṇappa
koṇṭappu nāyakkar makaṉāvār. ivar cakam 1520 (ki.pi. 1598)-il āṭip patiṉeṭṭām
perukkiṉ pōtu koḷḷiṭattil kūttap pirāṉ tīrttaṅ koṭuttaruḷutaṟkākak koḷḷiṭattiṉa
vaṭa kariyil tīrtta maṇṭapam kaṭṭiyuḷḷār.
----------------
7. marāṭṭiya maṉṉar
cāmpōji:-
civājiyiṉ mutal mintarākiya ivar ceñciyit talinakarākak koṇṭu koḷḷiṭattiṉ
vaṭapakutiyi āṇṭavar. ēṟakkuṟiya 38 āṇṭukaḷ kuṭumiyā maliyilum, maturiyilum
pātukāppāka vikkap peṟṟirunta naṭarācap perumāṉuṭiya tiruvuruvam ivarkālattil
tillic ciṟṟampalattiṟkuk koṇṭu varappeṟṟatu. ki. pi. 1684-il cepput takaṭu
vēyappeṟṟuk kuṭamuḻukku nikaḻntatu. ki. pi. 1686-il poṉvēyntu mīṇṭum
kuṭamuḻakku nikaḻttappeṟṟatu. tillip peruṅkōyil tiruppaṇiyi ivaruṭiya atikāri
'kōpālatātāji' eṉpavar kaṇkāṇittu niṟivēṟṟiṉār. tillicciṟṟampalat tavamuṉivar
eṉpār tillik kōyilil naṭarācapperumāṉ mīṇṭum eḻuntaruḷa utavi purintār.
aracaruṭiya kulakuruvākiya muttiyā tīṭcatar eṉ palar kumpāpiṣēkatti muṉniṉṟu
naṭattivittār. ivar ceyta tiruppaṇikaḷ tamiḻppalkalikkaḻakattāl veḷiyiṭap peṟṟa
'tañcimarāṭṭiyar ceppēṭukaḷ-50' eṉṉum nūlil 45 mutal 48 vari eṇṇiṭap peṟṟa
tiruvārūrc ceppēṭukaḷil eṭutturikkappeṟṟaṉa.
makamatiyar paṭiyeṭuppukkālattil hitar ali ik kōyilip pācaṟiyākakkoṇṭamiyālum,
ki.pi. 1749-il paṭittalivaṉ kōp eṉpavaṉ tēvikōṭṭikkup puṟaṅkāṭṭi ōṭivarumpōtu
tillikkōyilit taṉakku araṇākak koṇṭamiyālum, ki.pi. 1753-il pirañcukkārar
puvaṉakiriyik kippaṟṟit tillikkōyili araṇākak koṇṭu āṅkilēyarip puṟaṅkāṭṭi
yōṭacceytamiyālum ki.pi. 1780-il nikaḻnta micūryuttattil sr hayarvuṭ eṉpavar
ikkōyilip paṭi taṅkum iṭamākak koṇṭu etiriyit tākkiṉamiyā lum civarkkut talimik
kōyilākavuḷḷa it tillip peruṅkōyilāṉatu palavēṟu iṭipāṭukaḷiyaṭintatu. aḻakiya
ciṟpaṅkaḷ aḻivuṟṟaṉa āṅkilēyar āṭci in nāṭṭil nili peṟṟa piṉṉarē ipperuṅkōyilil
amitiyāṉa muṟiyil nāṭpūcaṉiyum tiruviḻākkaḷum muṉpōlat toṭarntu nikaḻvaṉavāyiṉa.
kāñcipuram pacciyappa mutaliyār tiruppaṇi
civaneṟic celvarākiya ivar, tamiḻakattiṟ ciṟappuṭiya kōyilkaḷ cilavaṟṟukku nipantam
aḷittuḷḷār. tillip patiyiṟ palanāṭkaḷ taṅkiyiruntu naṭarācapperumāṉi vaḻi paṭṭu
nāṭpūcaṉikkum tiruviḻākkaḷukkum aṟakkaṭṭaḷi niṟuviyuḷḷār. tillip peruṅkōyiliṉ
kiḻakkuk kōpurattip paḻutu pārttut tiruppaṇi ceytuḷḷār.
nakarattār tiruppaṇi
civamum tamiḻum vaḷartta taṉavaṇikac celvarkaḷākiya nakarattār cārpil tilli naṭarācap
perumāṉ tirukkōyil ceṉṟa pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil mikavum virivāṉa muṟiyil
tiruppaṇi ceyyap peṟṟatu. tilliyiṉ elliyil aṇṇāmalippalkalikkaḻakam niṟuviya
peruṅkoṭi vaḷḷal ceṭṭināṭṭaracar rājā car mu. aṇṇāmalic ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷuṭiya
tantiyār cā. rāma muttiya ceṭṭiyarvarkaḷ tillip peruṅkōyiliṉ mutaṟpirākārattiṉ
tirumāḷikippattiyiṉip putuppittum, tirumūlaṭṭāṉē cuvarar tirukkōyiliyum
umiyapārvati tirukkōyiliyum putuppittum,iraṇṭām pirākārattiyoṭṭi
aṇiveṭṭikkāl maṇṭapaṅkaḷik kaṭṭiyum, tillip poṉṉampalatti mēlum polivuṟac ceytum
ki.pi. 1891-ām āṇṭil kuṭa muḻukaku viḻāvic ciṟappuṟa nikaḻttiṉārkaḷ.
ivarkaṭkuppiṉ ivartam mintar rājā car. mu. aṇṇāmalic ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷ, ikkōyiliṉ
civaviṇava vēṟupāṭu akalat tillik kōvintarācap perumāḷ kōyilittiruppaṇi ceytu
naṭarācar cannitiyilum kōvittarācar cannitiyilum muṉmaṇṭapaṅkaḷik kaṭṭi ki.pi.
1934-ām āṇṭu tillik kōvintarācap perumāḷuk kuk kuṭamuḻukku viḻāc ceytārkaḷ.
cīrkāḻi capānāyaka mutaliyār tiruppaṇi
cīrkāḻi perunilakkiḻārākiya ivar, tilliyiṟ kaṉaka capiyil nāḷtōṟum
cantirameḷalīsvararkkum irattaṉa capāpatikkum nikaḻntuvarum tirumañcaṉatti aṉparkaḷ iruntu
taricippataṟku vacatiyākak kaṉakacapiyiṉ kiḻakkē tēkkumarakkūriyil cepputtakaṭu vēynta
taricaṉamaṉṟatti amittārkaḷ. ittiruppaṇiyi ivarkaṭkuppiṉ citampara nāta mutaliyār
avarkaḷ niṟivēṟṟiṉārkaḷ.
ikkālattiruppaṇi
ivvirupatām nūṟṟāṇṭil āṭur perunilakkiḻār taruma pūṣaṇam M.
irattiṉacapāpatippiḷḷiyavarkaḷum, citamparam aṇikalavaṇikar tarumapūṣaṇam ce. irattiṉacāmic
ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷum cērntu rūpā mūṉṟu ilaṭcattiṟku mēl celavuceytu tillic
ciṟcapiyiṉiyum kaṉakacapiyiṉiyum tiruppaṇi ceytu 1955-ām āṇṭil kuṭamuḻukku
viḻāvic ciṟappuṟa nikaḻttiṉar.
piṉṉar āṭūr perunilakkiḻār taruma pūṣaṇam M. irattiṉa capāpatip piḷḷiyavarkaḷāl
āyirakkāl maṇṭapat tiruppaṇi toṭaṅkic ceyyappeṟṟatu. avarkaḷukkuppiṉ avarkaḷ peyarāl
niṟuvappaṭṭa aṟakkaṭṭaḷiyiṉ cārpil paḻutu pārttu irumpuk kampikaḷāl cuṟṟilum
vaciyamikkap peṟṟut tiruppaṇi iṉitu niṟi vēṟiyatu.
citamparam tiru. W. kalyāṇa rāmapiḷḷi yavarkaḷāl toṭaṅkappeṟṟa civakāmiyammi
tirukkōyil tiruppaṇi avarkaṭkuppiṉ tiru. N.M. poṉṉampalam-piḷḷiyavarkaḷ talimiyil
tamiḻaka aracum ceṭṭi nāṭṭaracar rājā car muttiya ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷum potumakkaḷum
utaviya nitiyutaviyik koṇṭu niṟivu ceyyappeṟṟatu. 1972-il kuṭamuḻukku iṉitu
niṟivēṟiyatu.
citamparam nakaramaṉṟat talivarākavum pārāḷumaṉṟavuṟuppiṉarākavum caṭṭamaṉṟa-vuṟuppiṉarākavum
iruntu kūṭṭuṟavut tuṟi mutaliya palatuṟikaḷilum nāṭuvaḷam peṟa nalla pala paṇikaḷip
purinta R. kaṉakacapippiḷḷi avarkaḷ tam tantiyār āṭūr irattiṉacapātippiḷḷiyavarkaḷ
virumpiya vaṇṇam tillip peruṅkōyil tiruppaṇiyil īṭupāṭuṭiyarāyiṉar, avarkaḷit
talivarākavum muṉṉāḷ caṭṭamaṉṟa vuṟuppiṉar tiruppaṇiccelvar G. vākīcampiḷḷiyavarkaḷic
ceyalāḷarākavum koṇṭa citamparam capānāyakar tirumatil tiruppaṇikkuḻu
tillipperuṅkōyiliṉ veḷippuṟap peru matilākiya vīrappanāyakkarmatil tiruppaṇiyi
iṉitu niṟi vēṟṟiyatu. ikkuḻu 1972-il citamparam capānāyakar kōyil
tiruppaṇikkuḻuvāka māṟṟiyamikkappeṟṟatu. appoḻutu tamiḻaka araciṉ aṟaniliyattuṟi
āṇiyarākap paṇipurinta tiru.M.K. pālacuppiramaṇiyam avarkaḷatu utaviyuṭaṉ tillip
peruṅkōyil tiruppaṇiyic ciṟappuṟa nikaḻttutaṟku muppat tintulaṭca rūpā aḷavil
periya tiṭṭam (māsṭarpiḷāṉ) oṉṟu variyappaṭṭatu. ataṉpaṭi tamiḻaka araciṉ naṉkoṭi yāka
rūpā pattoṉpatu laṭcamum, potumakkaḷ naṉ koṭiyāka rūpā irupatulaṭcamum, paḻani
tēvattāṉam M.S kaṭṭaḷiyiṉ cārpil rājā car M.A. muttiyaceṭṭiyār kuṭumpattār,
ceṉṉi maruttuvar ṭākṭar irattiṉa vēl cuppiramaṇiyam ākiyōrmūlam rūpā paṉṉiraṇṭu
laṭcamum āka rūpā aimpatu laṭcattiṟku mēl celaviṭappaṭṭuc citamparam tirukkōyiliṉ
nāṉku kōpuraṅkaḷum tiru māḷikippattiyum mukkuṟuṇip-piḷḷiyār, kaṟpaka vināyakar,
cuppiramaṇiyar mutaliyamūrttikaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷum, cuṟṟuppirākāramum,nirutta capiyum.
pērampalamum, kaṉakacapiyum, āka ellāp pakutikaḷum tiruppaṇiceyyappeṟṟut taṉittaṉiyē
kuṭamuḻukku viḻā nikaḻattappeṟṟaṉa. ittiruppaṇikaḷiṉ niṟivākat tillik
kūttapperumāṉukku nikaḻum aṭcaya āṇṭu tittiṅkaḷ 29-ām nāḷ (11-2-1987)
putaṉkiḻami makākumpāpiṣēkam naṭipeṟavuḷḷatu. aṇmiyil naṭipeṟṟa ittiruppaṇikaḷāl
tilli naṭarācapperumāṉ tirukkōyililuḷḷa ellāc cannitikaḷum uṟpuṟa matilkaḷum
tirumāḷikip pattiyum āyirakkāl maṇṭapamum veḷippuṟamatilkaḷum tiruntiya muṟiyiṟ
paḻutupārkkap peṟṟup putiya polivuṭaṉ tikaḻtal kāṇalām.
----------------

8. tillic ciṟṟampalap peruṅkōyilil vaḷarnta kalikaḷ

kaṟṟakalviyilum iṉiyavaṉum, kalikkelām poruḷākiyavaṉum ākiya kaṇṇutaṟkaṭavuḷ
āṭalpuriyum tillipperuṅ kōyilāṉatu kaṭṭaṭakkali, ōviyakkali, ciṟpakkali āṭaṟkali,
icikkali mutaliya kalikaḷ palavaṟṟiyum vaḷartta taṉicciṟappuṭiyatākat tikaḻkiṉṟatu.
uḷḷattiṟku uvakiyaḷikkum kalikaḷuḷ āṭaṟkaliyum oṉṟu. itu iyal ici nāṭakameṉum
muttamiḻōṭum toṭarpuṭiyatu. ikkaliyāṉatu akakkūttu, puṟakkūttu patiṉōr āṭalkaḷ eṉap
palvēṟu pakutikaḷāka vaḷarkkap peṟṟatu. ētēṉum orukatiyiṉit taḻuvi naṭikkap peṟum
kūttiṉi nāṭakameṉṟum, kati taḻuvātu pāṭṭiṉatu poruḷi ṉukkēṟpak kikāṭṭi vallapañ
ceyyum cuviyum meyp pāṭum poruntiya avinayak kūttiṉi nāṭṭiyameṉavum vaḻaṅkutal
marapu. nāṭṭiyameṉpatu, āṭal pāṭal aḻaku eṉṉum mūvaki nalaṅkaḷiyum oruṅkē peṟṟa
āṭalmakaḷirāl tamatu akakkuṟippu uṭampil tōṉṟum meyppāṭukaḷāl puṟattē pulappaṭumpaṭi
cuvipeṟa naṭikkappeṟuvatu. itaṉ ilakkaṇattiṉi viritturippatu nāṭṭiya nūl. nāṭṭiyak
kaliyāṉatu ellām valla kūttapperumāṉatu aintoḻil tirukkūtti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu
tōṉṟi vaḷarntatākum. ikkaliyāṉatu ōviyam, ciṟpam, kāviyam, ākiya kalikaḷ palavaṟṟōṭum
toṭarpuṭiyatākum.
tillipperuṅkōyilāṉatu mēṟkuṟitta kalikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum vaḷarkkum ceḻuṅkali
niyamamākat tikaḻkiṉṟatu, kūttapperumāṉ uyirkaḷukku iṉ pantarum ulakiṉippaṭikka
vēṇṭum eṉṟa tiruvuḷḷattōṭu pēriṉpat tirukkūttiṉi āṭattoṭaṅkiṉār. avaratu
uṭukkiyiliruntē olikaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. avvolikaḷē eḻuttukkaḷuk kellām piṟappiṭam.
eṉavē eḻuttukkaḷi mutalākak koṇṭu iyaṅkum ellāmoḻikaḷum akkūttappērumāṉatu
uṭukki oliyiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiyaṉavēyām, tillicciṟṟampalavar eḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷa
ittirukkōyilil eḻunilikkōpuraṅkaḷ nāṉkilum iṟivaṉatu tirumēṉit tōṟṟaṅkaḷiyum
tēvar muṉivar mutaliyōr uruvaṅkaḷiyum eḻilpeṟa vaṭuttukkāṭṭum ciṟapaṅkaḷ pala iṭam
peṟṟiruttal kāṇalām.
teyvattiṟam vāynta kūttukkaḷāka nam tamiḻakattu neṭuṅkālamākap pōṟṟappeṟum ciṟappuṭiya
āṭalkaḷil pala ittirukkōyiṟ ciṟpaṅkaḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. civaperumāṉ āṭiya
nūṟṟeṭṭukkaraṇaṅkaḷum avipaṟṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkūṟum nāṭṭiya nūliṉ culōkaṅkaḷum mēlik
kōpura uḷvāyilil makaḷirāṭum muṟiyil viḷakkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟē teṟku, vaṭakku,
kiḻakku ākiya kōpuraṅkaḷilum ikkaraṇaṅkaḷiṉ ceyal muṟikaḷ ciṟpa amippil
veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷami kāṇalām. iṟivaṉukkuriyaṉavākac collappaṭṭa ikkaraṇaṅkaḷ,
eḻunilik kōpuram nāṉkiṉvāyilkaḷilum irupuṟamum tōḻiyar niṟka naṭuvē talimakaḷ
orutti āṭum muṟiyil amikkappeṟṟiruttal kāṇalām. iṅku naṭuniṉṟāṭum āṭal makaḷiṉ
vaṭivam ellāmvalla iṟiviyik kuṟittateṉak koḷvārumuḷar. ipperuṅkōyilil nirutta
capiyilum civakāmiyammi kōyiliṉ uṭpirakāramākiya tirumāḷikippattiyiṉ
kuṟaṭukaḷilum, pāṇṭiya nāyakam kōyiliṉ kuṟaṭukaḷilum amintuḷḷa ciṟpaṅkaḷ āṭaṟkaliyiṉ
palvēṟu karaṇaṅkaḷiyum pulappaṭuttu-vaṉavāka amintuḷḷaṉa. civakāmiyammi tirukkōyilil
āṭaṟkalikkuriya aṅkamāka aminta palvēṟu icik karuvikaḷiṉ amippum, avaṟṟi icivāṇarkaḷ
vācikkum muṟiyum ciṟpaṅkaḷāka vaṭittuk kāṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tōṟkaruvi tuṇikkaruvi
kañcakkaruvi narampukkaruvi eṉappaṭum icikkaruvikaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷil ippoḻutu vaḻakkil
illātaṉa cilavum iṭam peṟṟiruttal kāṇalām. 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil makamatiyar mutaliya
puṟaccamayap paṭivīrarkaḷ kāyilip pācaṟiyākak koṇṭu taṅkiṉamiyāl. ik ciṟpaṅkaḷiṟ pala
citivuṟṟaṉa. vīṇi, yāḻ mutaliya narampuka karuvikaḷ viralāl mīṭṭi vācittaṟkuriyaṉa
eṉpataṉip palarum aṟivar. civakāmiyammi kōyiliṟ kāṇappaṭum narampuk karuvi oṉṟu
ikkālattil vaḻaṅkum mēlināṭṭu icik karuviyāṉa piṭilip pōṉṟu, villiṉāl
vācittaṟkuriya karuviyāka amintuḷḷami kāṇalām. ikkaruviyiṉ muḻu amippiṉiyum nām
kāṇa muṭiyātapaṭi kalakakkārarkaḷāl, itu citivuṟṟuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tillikkōyilil kutiri pūṭṭiya tērmaṇṭapamāka nirutta capiyum, yāṉiyum yāḷiyum
iḻukkum tēr amippākap pāṇṭiya nāyakam tirukkōyilum amintuḷḷami paḻaṅkālak kaṭṭaṭak
kaliyiṉ ciṟappiṉi viḷakkuvatākum. ivvirumaṇṭapaṅkaḷil uḷḷa tūṇkaḷ yāvum, muḻuvatum
teyvat tiruvuruviṉit taṉṉakattē koṇṭa ciṟu ciṟu tērkaḷip peṟṟaṉavāka viḷaṅkutal
akkālac ciṟpikaḷiṉ nuṇṇiya vēlippāṭṭut tiṟamikku, or eṭuttukkāṭṭākum.
tillip peruṅkōyil, neṭuntuārattilēyē oḷiyuṭaṉ tikaḻum cempoṉ māḷikiyākiya
poṉṉam palattiṉit taṉṉakattē koṇṭu aḻakiya ciṟpaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟiyuṭiya tāy amintiruntatu
eṉpataṉiyum, ataṉ naṭuvē māṇikkak kūttaṉa āṭal purintaruḷum tillic ciṟṟampalamākiya
tirumaṉṟam aḻakiya ciṟanta oviyaṅkaḷ pala eḻutappeṟṟuk kaviṉuṟattikaḻntatu eṉpataṉiyum
“ciṟpantikaḻtaru tiṇmatiṟṟilli” (tirukkōviyār.315) eṉavum, “cēṇiṟpoli cempoṉ
māḷikit tillic ciṟṟampalattu māṇikkak kūttaṉ” (tirukkōviyār 23) eṉavum,
'ciṟṟampalattu eḻutum oviyaṅkaṇṭaṉṉa oṇṇutalāḷ'(tiruk-384) eṉavum varum toṭarkaḷil
maṇivācakap perumāṉ kuṟittup pōṟṟiyuḷḷami, avar kālattilēyē tillic ciṟṟampalam
ciṟpam oviyam mutaliya kalikaḷukku nilikkaḷaṉākat tikaḻnta ceytiyip
pulappaṭuttuvatākum.
tillit tirucciṟṟampalat tirukkōyilil vaḻipāṭṭuk kālaṅkaḷil nāḷtōṟum kuṭamuḻā
muḻakkappeṟṟatu. icceytiyiṉi, 'ciṟṟampalattu aṭiyēṉ kaḷitarak kārmiṭaṟṟōṉ naṭamāṭak
kaṇṇār muḻavam tuḷitarak kāreṉa ārttaṉa' (tirukkōviyār 324) eṉavarum tiruk kōviyār
toṭarāl aṟiyalām. civapāta cēkaraṉ eṉap pōṟṟappeṟum irājarāja cōḻaṉāl tirumuṟi kaṇṭu
eṭukkappaṭṭatum mūvar aruḷiya iyalicit tamiḻākiya it tiruppatikaṅkaḷukkut tirunīlakaṇṭa
yāḻppāṇar marapil tōṉṟiya ammiyār oruvarik koṇṭu paṇṭupōl iciyamittaṟkuriya aruḷ
niliyamāka amintatum it tillip peruṅ kōyilēyākum.
“narampuṭi yāḻ oli muḻaviṉ kātavoli vētoli
arampiyartaṅ kīta oli aṟāttilli”
      (periya taṭuttāṭ-9)
eṉac cēkkiḻār perumāṉ iyalici nāṭakameṉum muttamiḻ vaḷarccikkum it
tillipperuṅkōyil nilikkaḷamāy aminta tiṟatti viritturittuḷḷami iṅku oppu
nōkkattakuvatākum.
civakāmiyammi tirukkōyilil muṉ maṇṭapak kūriyil variyap peṟṟuḷḷa oviyaṅkaḷ mikavum
paḻami vāyntaṉa. pāṇṭiya nāyakam tirukkōyiliṉ mēṟkūriyil eḻutap peṟṟa oviyaṅkaḷum
ittirukkōyiliṉ piṟaviṭaṅkaḷil aṇmiyileḻutap peṟṟa oviyaṅkaḷum putumip polivu
peṟṟut tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. ittirukkōyilil aminta ēḻunilik kōpuraṅkaḷ nāṉkum ikkālak
kaṭṭaṭap poṟiyāḷar palarum viyantupārāṭṭum muṟiyil amintuḷḷami kāṇalām.
tillip peruṅ kōyil eḻunilikkōpuraṅkaḷ nāṉkiṉuḷḷum kiḻakkuk kōpuram, tarimaṭṭam
mutal viyāḷam vari 35 aṭi uyaramum 108 aṭi nīḷamum 60 aṭi akalamum uṭiya
karuṅkaṟkaṭṭiṭamum, ataṟkumēl viyāḷam mutal kalacam vari 15 aṭi uyaram uṭiya eḻunili
māṭaṅkaḷum āka 152 uyara muṭiyatu. ataṉmēl 7 aṭi 6 aṅkulam uyaramuṭiya 13
ceppukkalacaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. iraṇṭām kōpperuñciṅkaṉāl amikkappeṟṟa ikkōpuravāyiliṉ vaṭa
puṟattu māṭattilē teṟku nōkkiya niliyil, ivaṉatu uruvam iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.
ikkōpuratti munnūṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉ paḻutupārttut tiruppaṇi ceytavar kāñcipuram
pacciyappa mutaliyārāvār.
teṟkuk kōpuram tarimaṭṭam mutal vimāṉam vari 35 aṭi uyaramum 108 aṭi nīḷamum 62 aṭi
akalamum uṭiya karuṅkaṟkaṭṭiṭamum, ataṉ mēl vimāṉam mutal kalacam vari 110 aṭi uyaramuḷḷa
eḻunili māṭappakutiyum āka 142 aṭi uyaramuṭiyatu. mēluḷḷa 13 ceppuk kalacaṅkaḷum 8
aṭi 3 aṅkulam uyaramuṭiyaṉa.
mēṟkuk kōpuram tarimaṭṭam mutal vimāṉam vari 37 aṭi uyaramum 102 aṭi nīḷamum 60 aṭi
akalamum uṭiya karuṅkaṟ kaṭṭiṭap pakutiyum, ataṉ mēl vimāṉam mutal kalacam vari 115 aṭi
uyaramuṭiya eḻunili māṭamum āka 145 aṭi uyaramuṭiyatu. itaṉ mēluḷḷa 13
ceppukkalacaṅkaḷum 5 aṭi 9 aṅkulam uyaramuṭiyaṉa. ikkōpuratti amittavaṉ caṭiyavarmaṉ
cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ. ātalāl itucuntara pāṇṭiyaṉ tirukkōpuram eṉak kalveṭṭukkaḷiṟ
kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. ik kōpura vāyiliṉ teṉpuṟamāṭattilē vaṭakku nōkki vaḻipaṭum
niliyil ippāṇṭiyaṉatu uruvam iṭam peṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām.
vaṭakkuk kōpuram tari maṭṭam mutal vimāṉam vari 40 aṭi uyaramum 108 aṭi nīḷamum 70
aṭi akalamum uṭiya karuṅkaṟkaṭṭiṭap pakutiyum ataṉ mēl vimāṉam mutal kalacam vari 107
aṭi uyaramuṭiya eḻunilimāṭap pakutiyum āka 147 aṭi uyaramuṭiyatu. ataṉ mēl 7 aṭi
11 aṅkula uyaram vāynta 13 ceppuk kalacaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
mēṟkuṟitta eḻunilikkōpuraṅkaḷ nāṉkiṉ vāyiṟpaṭikaḷiṉ nilikaḷikkāṇpōr
tamiḻakakkaṭṭiṭakkalivallunar mēṟkoṇṭa poṟiyiyal vaḷarccikku eṭuttukkāṭṭāka it tillip
peruṅkōyil viḷaṅkuvataṉi naṉkuṇarvar. ivvāṟu tamiḻakattiṉ kaṭṭiṭakkali, ciṟpakkali,
nāṭṭiyak kali, icikkali, ōviyakkali mutaliya kalikaḷ palavaṟṟiṉ vaḷarccikkum
nilikkaḷamāka aminta tillip peruṅkōyiliṉ kalinayaṅkaḷip pēṇippātukāttal tamiḻaka
aracukkum intiya aracukkum tamiḻ makkaḷukkum uriya taliyāya kaṭamiyākum.
----------------

9. tillikkōyiliṉ nirvākam

tillip peruṅkōyiliṉ nirvākamaṉittum nāṭāḷum maṉṉaṉatu nēraṭippārviyil
nikaḻntamiyāl ittalam, kalveṭṭukkaḷil taṉiyūr perumpaṟṟappuliyūr eṉa vaḻaṅkap
peṟuvatāyiṟṟu. (te. i. ka. tokuti IV eṇ 226) aracaṉuṭiya atikāri oruvarō
iruvarō ittirukkōyiliṉ naṭimuṟiyi, tilliyil taṅkik kavaṉittu varuvār. kōyil
paṇikaḷip purintu varum palvēṟu kuḻuviṉarum mūlapparuṣiyārum nakaramakkaḷum
ivvatikārikaḷuṭaṉ kalantu kōyilukkuriya nilamutaliya uṭimikaḷip pātukākkum
poṟuppiṉi mēṟkoḷvar. icceyti, "toṇṭimāṉum tiruvi yāṟuṭiyāṉum maturāntakap
piramarāyaṉum āḷuṭiyār kōyilukkuc camutāyat tirumāḷikik kūṟu tilliyampalap
pallavarāyaṉum cīkāriyañ ceyvārkaḷum camutāyañ ceyvārkaḷum kōyil nāyakañ ceyvārkaḷum
tirumāḷikik kūṟu ceyya tiruvāy moḻintaruḷiṉa paṭi" (te.i. ka, to. IV eṇ 222.)
eṉa varum aracu āṇiyāl naṉkupulaṉākum. mutaṟ kōpperuñciṅkaṉ āṭciyil
tillipperuṅkōyil avaṉatu nērppārviyil nirvākañ ceyyappeṟṟatākat terikiṉṟatu.
iraṇṭām kōpperuñ ciṅkaṉ kālattil uṭiyār tirucciṟṟampala muṭiyār kōyili "srī
mākēcuvarak kaṇkāṇi ceyvārkaḷum srīkāriyam ceyvārkaḷum kōyil nāyakañ ceyvārkaḷum
tirumāḷikik kūṟu ceyvārkaḷum kaṇakkarum" (te. i. ka, to. XII eṇ 148) aracaṉatu
āṇippaṭi kaṇkāṇittu vantaṉar eṉat terikiṉṟatu. ikkōyiliṉ nila mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiya
cāṉṟukaḷ perumpaṟṟap puliyūr mūlaparuṣiyār kiyeḻuttuṭaṉ tirukkiyoṭṭip
paṇṭārattil oṭukkap peṟṟiruntaṉa. tillipperuṅ kōyilil variyappeṟṟup paṭiyeṭukkap
peṟṟuḷḷa irunūṟṟeḻupatu kalveṭṭukkaḷil ikkōyiliṉ nirvākam nāṭāḷ vēntaṉiṉ
āṇippaṭi tirukkōyilil paṇiceyyum palvēṟu kuḻuviṉarālum camutāyañ ceyvārākiya
nakara makkaḷālum nirvakikkap peṟṟu vanta ceytiyē iṭam peṟṟuḷḷami maṉam koḷḷat
takuvatākum. puṟaccamaya makkaḷ paṭiyeṭuppiṉāl ikkōyiṟ pūci taṭippaṭṭa niliyil
kūttapperumāṉ tiruvuruvattiyum iṉṟiyamiyāta piṟa uṭimikaḷiyum pātukākkum
poṟuppil ikkōyiliṟ pūci puriyum tillivāḻantaṇarkaḷ, tam kaṭamiyāka mēṟ koṇṭu
oḻukiṉamiyālum, mika nerukkaṭiyāṉa kālattil uyirttiyākam ceytu tirukkōyil
uṭimikaḷip pātu kāttamiyālum ikkōyiliṉ nirvākattip piṟkālattil tillip
potuvār eṉappaṭum tillivāḻantaṇar kavaṉittu varuvārāyiṉar.
mutalām carapōciyiṉ āṭciyil ki.pi. 1719-il ēkōci yaracarāṟ ciṟappuc ceyyappeṟṟa
toṇṭi maṇṭala vēḷāḷarum cēṟikiḻār kōttirattiṉarum, niyamam eṉṟa ūriṉ talivarum ākiya
rāyasrī mutaliyār muttuc cellappa mutaliyār tarumamākac civappēraṉpar tāṇṭavamūrtti
cēru vikārar eṉpār vallattuttōṭṭamum oru āḷ āyattīrviyum tillic
citamparēcuvaracuvāmikkum tillikkōvintarāyap perumāḷukkum tāṉa cācaṉam ceytu
citamparam akōra paṇṭāram cārpilē ariciyappa mutaliyār kaṭṭaḷiyiṉi naṭattivarum tāṇṭavaṉ
tōṭṭam caravaṇittampirāṉukku nīrvārttukkoṭuttār eṉpatum, citamparam capāpatik
kaṭṭaḷiyiṉi naṭattivarum caravaṇittampirāṉ eṉpavar it tāṉa cācaṉatti ēṟṟukkoṇṭu
cūriyacantirar uḷḷa variyum ikkaṭṭaḷiyiṉi naṭattuvatāka oppukkoṇṭār eṉpatum
tamiḻppalkalikkaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa 'tañci marāṭṭiya ceppēṭukaḷ - 50' eṉṟa nūlil 7-ām
eṇṇuḷḷa ' 'tiruvārūrcceppēṭu-1'- il kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. (pakkam 55-61)
ki.pi. 25-8-1719-il aḷikkappaṭṭa. ittamiḻc cep pēṭu citamparam - kōyilukku
uriyatākum. tiruvārūrk kōyiliṟ pātukākkappaṭṭiruttalāl, tiruvārūrc ceppēṭu,
eṉṟa peyaril veḷiyiṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu. icceppēṭṭiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa aṟakkaṭṭaḷiyiṉi
ēṟṟu naṭattum poṟup puṭiyavarākavō ikkaṭṭaḷikkuriya nirvākattiṟku uriyavarākavō
tīṭcitarkaḷ icceppēṭṭiṟ kuṟikkappaṭavilli. ivvaṟakkaṭṭaḷiyiṉi ēṟṟu naṭattum nirvākap
poṟuppuṭiyavarākat tāṇṭavaṉ tōṭṭam capāpatikaṭṭaḷiyiṉi naṭattum caravaṇittampirāṉ eṉpavarē
kuṟippiṭappeṟṟuḷḷār.
icceppēṭṭiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa - ceytiyiṉik kūrntu nōkkuṅkāl akkālattil tillip
peruṅkōyilukku maṉṉarkaḷālum kuṭimakkaḷālum niṟuvappaṭum kaṭṭaḷikaḷi nirvakikkum
poṟuppu akōrapaṇṭāram pōṉṟa tirumaṭattiṉ talivarkaḷiṭattilum caravaṇit tampirāṉ
talivarkaḷiṭattilum caravaṉit tampirāṉ pōṉṟa tuṟavikaḷiṭattilum oppaṭikkappaṭṭa teṉpatum
tilli varaḻantaṇarkaḷ tirukkōyiliṉ pūcaṉi muṟikaḷākiya uḷ tuṟi aluvalkaḷi maṭṭumē
kavaṉittukkoḷḷum urimiyāḷarāka iruntaṉar eṉpatum naṉku pulaṉātal kāṇalām.
tillipperuṅkōyilukkeṉa - vēntarkaḷum kuṟunila maṉṉārkaḷum, vaḷḷalkaḷum,
tēvatāṉamākak koṭuttuḷḷa ūrkaḷum nilaṅkaḷum piṟkālattavarkaḷāṟ cuvarappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē.
tillip peruṅkōyilukkeṉa ippoḻutu nila vuṭimi etuvumilli. kāñcipuram
pacciyappamutaliyār pōṉṟatamiḻ muṉṉōrkaḷ niṟuviyuḷḷa aṟakkaṭṭaḷi cuḷik koṇṭē
ikkōyiliṉ nāḷvaḻipāṭum tiruviḻākkaḷum ciṟappuṟa nikaḻntu varukiṉṟaṉa.
tillivāḻantaṇarkaḷ kūttapperumāṉukkup pūcaṉi puriyum avvaḷavil maṭṭum
amintuviṭāmal ikkōyiluḷḷē nikaḻum akattoṇṭukaḷ evlāvaṟṟiyum tāmē ceyyum
poṟuppiṉi - mēṟkoṇṭavarkaḷ eṉpatu 'antaṇar kōyi luḷḷāl akampaṭit toṇṭu ceyvār.'
eṉavarum cēkkiḻār vāy moḻiyālum, kuṭantiyi yaṭuttuḷḷa irācarācēccurat
tirukkōyililuḷḷa tilli vāḻantaṇarkaḷik kuṟitta tiruttoṇṭat tokiyaṭiyār
ciṟpattiṉālum iṉitu pulaṉākum. tilliyampalap perumāṉit tamatu vāḻvākavum vippu
nitiyākavum koṇṭu vaḻipāṭu ceytu varum nāṉmaṟi yantaṇarkaḷākiya ittillivāḻ
antaṉarkaḷi 'ūṟuiṉ tamiḻāl uyarntār' eṉat tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷiyār ciṟappittup
pōṟṟiyuḷḷār. piṉḷiyār aruḷiya vaṇṇam tamiḻāl uyarnta tillivāḻantaṇarkaḷ tillik
kaṉakacapiyivē centamiḻc civat tirumuṟikaḷip paṇṇuṭaṉ ōtip pōṟṟi varuvatu tamiḻ makkaḷ
ellōrum pārāṭṭutaṟkuriya tiruppaṇiyākum.
"akaliṭat tuyartta tilli yantaṇar akilamellām
pukaḻtiru maṟiyōr eṉṟum potu naṭam pōṟṟivāḻka"
eṉac cēkkiḻār perumāṉ vāḻttiya aruṇmoḻi vāḻttu tillivāḻantaṇarkku eṉṟum
uriyatākum.
----------

10. piṟcērkki

1. tilli naṭarācar tirukkōyilil kōvintarācap perumāḷ iṭam peṟṟa varalāṟu
centamiḻ nūlkaḷilum vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷilum tillip peruṅkōyil civacamayattārkkuc
ciṟappurimiyuṭiya talimik kōyilākavē pōṟṟap peṟṟuḷḷatu. viyākkira pātarākiya
pulikkāl muṉivar tirumūlaṭṭāṉap perumāṉi vaḻipaṭṭup pōṟṟiṉamiyāl perum
paṟṟappuliyūr eṉavum, tillivaṉamākiya ittiruttalattilē patañcali muṉivar ceyta
tavattiṟku aruḷkūrntu ellām valla civaperumāṉ ñāṉa mayamāṉa ampalattilē āṉantak kūttu
nikaḻttiyaruḷutalāl tillic ciṟṟampalam eṉavum vaḻaṅkap peṟuvatu ittillip peruṅ
kōyilākum. tiruñāṉacampantar, tiru nāvukkaracar, cuntarar ākiya mūvarum aruḷiya
tēvārat tirup patikaṅkaḷum, māṇikkavācakar aruḷiya tiruvācakam tirucciṟṟampalak
kōviyākiya aruḷ nūlkaḷum tirumūlar tirumantiram mutal tiruttoṇṭar purāṇam
īṟākavuḷḷa ēṉiya tirumuṟikaḷum tillicciṟṟampalattiyē civa talaṅkaḷellāvaṟṟilum
mutaṉmiyuṭiyatākak koṇṭu'kōyil' eṉṉum peyarāṟ ciṟappittup pōṟṟiyuḷḷaṉa. cēracōḻa
pāṇṭiyarkaḷākiya tamiḻ vēntar āṭciyilum iṭikkālattil vanta pallava maṉṉarkaḷ āṭciyilum
piṟkālattil vanta vicayanakara maṉṉar nāyakkamaṉṉar marāṭṭiya maṉṉar āṭciyilum tillip
peruṅkōyil kūttap perumāṉukkuriya tirukkōyilākavē kalveṭṭukkaḷiṟ kuṟikkap
peṟṟuḷḷatu. ittirukkōyiliṉ āṭciyurimikaḷ aṉittum civaṉaṭiyāākaḷākiya
mātēsvararkaḷukkum kōyilil srī kāriyamākiya akattoṇṭu purintu varum tilli
vāḻantaṇar mutaliyōrkkum kōyil nāyakañ ceyvār ākiya araciyalatikārikaṭkum camutāyañ
ceyvārākiya ūrp potumakkaḷukkum uriyaṉavākak kōyilil variyappeṟṟa kalveṭṭukkaḷiṟ
kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. citamparam naṭarācar kōyilil, variyap peṟṟuḷḷa 270 kku mēṟpaṭṭa
kalveṭṭukkaḷil ikkōyil uṭiyār tirucciṟṟampalamuṭiyarākiya poṉṉampalavāṇarkkuc
ciṟappurimi yuṭiya tirukkōyilākak kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām.
ivvāṟu civa camayattārkkē ciṟappurimi vāynta citamparam naṭarācap perumāṉ
tirukkōyilil kōvintarācap perumāḷ tiruvuruvam eppoḻutu piratiṣṭi ceyyappaṭṭatu
eṉpatum ikkōyilil srīviṣṇavarkaḷukku ettakiya urimiyuṇṭu eṉpatum varalāṟṟu muṟiyil
nōkkuvatu ikkōviliṉ amitiyāṉa naṭimuṟikku mikavum uṟutuṇi ceyvatākum.
tēvāra āciriyar mūvar kālam variyilum tillipperuṅ kōyilil kōvintarācapperumāḷ
canniti iṭampeṟa villi. citamparam capānāyakar tirukkōyilil kōvinta rācapperumāḷi
mutaṉ mutaṟ piratiṣṭi ceytavaṉ ki.pi. 726 mutal 775 vari āṭcipurinta
nantivarmapallavaṉ āvaṉ, icceyti tiruccittirakūṭattip pōṟṟit tirumaṅki yāḻvār
pāṭiyaruḷiya periya tirumoḻiyāṟ pulaṉākiṉṟatu.
“pim poṉṉum muttum maṇiyuṅ koṇarntu
paṭimaṉṉavaṉ pallavarkōṉ paṇinta
cempoṉ maṇimāṭaṅkaḷ cūḻnta tillit
tiruc cittira kūṭam ceṉṟu cērmiṉkaḷē"
      (periya tirumoḻi 3-2-3)
eṉat tirumaṅkiyāḻvār pāṭip pōṟṟutalāl tillip peruṅkōyililuḷḷa
kōvintarācapperumāḷ canniti nanti varma pallavaṉāl mutaṉ mutal amikkappeṟṟa teṉpatu
naṉku pulaṉākum. iccanniti tillik kūttappirāṉ tirumuṟṟattil ciṟiya
tiṇṇiyaḷaviltāṉ mutaṉ mutal niṟuvappeṟṟiruntatu eṉpatu tillit tiruccittirakūṭam
eṉṟa peyarāl teḷiyappaṭum. 'cittirakūṭam teṟṟiyampalam' eṉpatu tivākaram. teṟṟi --
tiṇṇi, eṉavē tiṇṇiyaḷavilaminta ciṟiya iṭattilēyē nantivarmapallavaṉ
kōvintarācapperumāḷip piratiṣṭi ceytāṉ eṉṟu terikiṉṟatu.
civaṉit toḻukulamākap peṟṟu ammutalvaṉatu ūrtiyākiya iṭapattiṉi ilacciṉiyākak
koṇṭavarkaḷ pallava maṉṉarkaḷ. attakiya pallavamarapilē tōṉṟiya nantivarma pallavaṉ taṉ āṭciyiṉ
muṟpakutiyil civa viṇava camayaṅkaḷiṟ camanōkkuṭiyaṉāka viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. ivaṉ taṉkālattil
tirumāliṉ tiruvaruḷukkuriya aruḷāciriyarākat tikaḻnta tirumaṅkiyāḻvāratu
toṭarpiṉāl paramaviṇavaṉāka māṟi viṭṭāṉ. "mukuntaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷit tavira vēṟoṉṟiṟkum
avaṉ talivaṇaṅkavilli” eṉattaṇṭattōṭṭap paṭṭayam immaṉṉaṉip paṟṟik kūṟutalāl ivaṉ viṇavaṉāka
māṟiya ceyti naṉku pulaṉākum. ivaṉ parama viṇavaṉāka māṟiya piṉpē tillic ciṟṟampalap
peruṅkōyil muṟṟattiṟ kōvintarācap perumāḷip piratiṣṭi ceytaṉaṉ eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
tillit tiruccittira kūṭattil piratiṣṭi ceyyap peṟṟa kōvintarācap perumāḷi
muṟippaṭi pūci ceytu vantavarkaḷ tilli mūvāyirar eṉac ciṟappitturikkappaṭum
tillivāḻantaṇarkaḷēyāvar. iṅkuḷḷa perumāḷ tiruvuruvam naṭarācarkōyilic cārnta
cuṟṟukkōyil teyvam (parivārateyvam) eṉṟa aḷavilēyē tillivāḻantaṇarkaḷāl muṟippaṭi
pūcaṉi ceyyappeṟṟu vantatu. ivvāṟē kāñcinakaril ēkāmparanātar tirukkōyivil parivāra
teyvamāka eḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷa perumāḷi "nilāttiṅkaḷ tuṇṭattāṉ” eṉat tirumaṅkiyāḻvār
maṅkaḷācācaṉam ceytiruppatum iṅku eḻuntaruḷiya perumāḷip pūcaṉi ceyyum urimiyiṉi
ikkōyiṟ pūci muṟiyiṉarākiya āti civak kurukkaḷ iṉṟaḷavum mēṟkoṇṭiruppatum iṅku
niṉittaṟkuriyatākum. tillik kōvintarācapperumāḷ piratiṣṭi paṟṟiyum ātiyilirunta
pūci muṟipaṟṟiyum ārāycciyaṟiñar mu. irākaviyaṅkār avarkaḷ 'tiruccittirakūṭam'
eṉṉum ārāyccik kaṭṭuriyil piṉ varumāṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār:
tirumaṅki maṉṉaṉ
"pim poṉṉum muttum maṇiyuṅ koṇarntu
paṭimaṉṉaṉ pallavarkōṉ paṇinta
      -- tillit tiruccittirakūṭam" (pe.ti. 3-2-3)
eṉṟu pāṭukiṉṟār. itaṉāl pallava vēntaṉoruvaṉāl ātiyil apimāṉikkappaṭṭatu
ittirumālkōyil eṉpatu teriyavarum.
"pallava malliyar kōṉpaṇinta paramēccura viṇṇakaram",
"nantipaṇiceytanakar nantipuraviṇṇakaram"       (pe.ti' 5-19-7)
kōccōḻaṉ cērnta kōyil tirunaṟiyūr maṇimāṭam” (6, 5, 5.9)
eṉap paṇṭiyaracarkaḷ tiruppaṇiceytu vaṇaṅkiya talaṅkaḷi ivvāṟē āḻvārkaḷ pāṭiyiruttal
kāṇalām.
'paṇinta kōyil' mutaliyaṉa, paṇiceytu, piratiṣṭitta kōyil eṉṟa poruḷil muṉpu
vaḻaṅkiyavi eṉpatu; mēṟ kāṭṭiya āḻvārkaḷ vākkukaḷiṉiṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum.
pallavarkōṉāl paṇiceytu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu ittiruc cittirakūṭam eṉṟu karututal
poruntum. iṅkaṉam paṇinta pallavaṉākat tirumaṅkiyāḻvārāṟ pukaḻappaṭṭavaṉ avar kālattē
parama viṣṇavaṉāka viḷaṅkiya iraṇṭām nanti varmaṉākac collalām.
ic cittirakūṭat tirumāli muṟkālattil muṟippaṭi ārātittu vantavarkaḷ tilli
mūvāyiravarēyāvar.
"mūvāyiranāṉ maṟiyāḷar muṟiyāl vaṇaṅka
--tēvātitēvaṉ tikaḻkiṉṟa tillit tiruccittirakūṭam"
      (pe.ti. 3.2-8)
"tillinakart tiruccittira kūṭantaṉṉuḷ
antaṇarkaḷ oru mūvāyirava rētta
aṇimaṇiyā caṉattirunta vammāṉ"
      (kula. ti.10,2)
eṉṟa āḻvārkaḷ vākkukkaḷāl itu teriyavarukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟāl ciṟṟampalamāṉa civālaya
vaḻipāṭṭiyum, teṟṟiyampalamāṉa cittirakūṭa vaḻipāṭṭiyum, muṟippaṭi purittu
vantavarkaḷ tilli mūvāyiravar eṉpatu viḷakkamām" (ceṉṉip palkalik kīḻttici moḻi
ārāyccittuṇar tokuti III {1938-39) pakuti 1) eṉac ciṟanta ārāycciyāḷarum
srīviṣṇavarumāṉa rāvcākēp mu. irākaviyaṅkāravarkaḷ kāytluvattalakaṟṟi naṭu niṉṟu kūṟiya
āyvu muṭivukaḷ aṟiñarkaḷatu pārāṭṭukku uriyaṉavākum.
tillip peruṅ kōyilil iṭikkālattil nantivarma pallavaṉāl piratiṭṭi ceyyappeṟṟa
kōvintarācap perumāḷukkuc civacamayattavarākiya tillimūvāyiravar pūci ceytu
varuvataṉik kaṇṭu maṉam poṟukkāta piṟkāla vīra viṣṇavarkaḷiṟ cilar. ciṟukac ciṟukat
tillit tirucciṟṟampalak kōyil naṭimuṟikaḷukkut tolliyuṇṭākki vantaṉar. avarkaḷāṟ
ceyyappaṭṭuvarum tollikaḷ nāḷukku nāḷ atikarittaṉa. avaṟṟāl tillicciṟṟampalat
tiruppaṇikaḷum nāṭpūcaṉikaḷum taṭippaṭuvaṉavāyiṉa. atukaṇṭu maṉam, poṟāta iraṇṭāṅ
kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ ivviṇavarkaḷ ceyyum tollikaḷuk kellām oru kāraṇamāka vuḷḷatu
kōvintarācapperumāḷ mūrttamēyeṉa eṇṇi ataṉit tillip peruṅkōyiliṉiṉṟum
appuṟappaṭuttiṉaṉ eṉpataṉi ivaṉuṭiya avikkaḷap pulavarākiya kavic cakkaravartti oṭṭakkūttar
tāmpāṭiya ulāvilum takkayākapparaṇiyilum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
iraṇṭām kulōttuṅkaṉ tillikkōyiliṉiṉṟum tirumāl mūrttatti appuṟappaṭuttiya
icceyalip piṟ kālattil viṇavarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭa tivyacūricaritam, kōyiloḻuku mutalāṉa
nūlkaḷ mikippaṭuttikkūṟi iv vēntaṉ mītu aṭāta paḻikaḷic cumattiyum, kirumikaṇṭa
cōḻaṉ eṉa ivaṉi iḻittuk kūṟiyum uḷḷaṉa. innūlkaḷil ivvēntaṉikkuṟittuk
kūṟappaṭuvaṉavellām veṟuṅ kaṟpaṉik kaṉatakaḷē; uṇmiyāvaṉa alla eṉpatu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ
tuṇipākum. (piṟkālac cōḻar carittiram, aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻaka veḷiyīṭu, pakkam
95,96 pārkka).
iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅkaṉ āṭcikkālattil tillik kōyililiruntu appuṟappaṭuttappaṭṭa
kōvintarācapperumāḷ tiruvuruvatti irāmānucar kīḻit tiruppatiyiṟ koṇṭu cērttu
aṅkē piratiṭṭi ceytār eṉa irāmānuja tivya cariti kūṟukiṟatu. irāmānucarāl
piratiṭṭi ceyyappaṭṭa tiruvuruvam iṉṟum aṅkēyuḷḷateṉpatum, ippōtu citamparam
kōyililuḷḷa kōvintarācap perumāḷ tiruvuruvam vicayanakara aracar accutarāyar kālattil
putitākap piratiṭṭi ceyyappaṭṭateṉpatum varatarāja aiyaṅkār pāṭiya pākavatapurāṇattil
tiruvaraṅkappaṭalattiluḷḷa 63, 99 ām ceyyuṭkaḷāl naṉku pulaṉākum. eṉavē iraṇṭāṅ
kulōttuṅkacōḻaṉ āṭcikkālamākiya ki.pi. paṉṉiraṇṭām nāṟṟāṇṭiṟkum accutarāyar
āṭcikkālamākiya ki.pi.patiṉāṟām nuāṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭippaṭṭakālattilē citamparam
naṭarācapperumāṉ kōyilil kōvintarācap perumāḷ mūrttam illiyeṉṟu terikiṟatu.
ki.pi 1251 mutal 1271 vari āṭcipurintavarum civaviṇavacamayaṅkaḷ iraṇṭiṉiyum oppa
matittup pōṟṟiyavarum 'āmuttamālyatā' eṉṉum nūli iyaṟṟi nācciyārākiya āṇṭāḷip
pōṟṟiyavarum srīviṣṇavarum ākiya vicayanakar maṉṉar kiruṣṇatēvarāyar, ki.pi. 1516-il
poṭṭaṉūril tām peṟṟa veṟṟikku aṭiyāḷamākat tilli naṭarācapperumāṉ tirukkōyiliṉ
vaṭakkuk kōpurattikkaṭṭi muṭittup poṉṉampala vāṇarkku nilam vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār (te. i. ka
tokuti VII eṇ 149). itaṉi yuṟṟunōkkuṅkāl kiruṣṇatēvarāyar kālattil kōvintarācap
perumāḷ cannitiyilliyeṉpatum, tillip peruṅkōyil poṉṉampala nātarākiya
civaperumāṉukkē ciṟappuṭiya tirukkōyil eṉṉum karuttuṭiyavar kiruṣṇa tēvarāyar
eṉpatum naṉku viḷaṅkum.
kiruṣṇa tēvarāyarukkup piṉ ki.pi 1529-il paṭṭattukku vanta accutatēvarāyar ki. pi
1539-il tilli naṭarācar kōyilil kōvintarācap perumāṉi mīṇṭum piratiṣṭi ceytu
vikānaca cūttirattiṉpaṭi pūcinaṭakka 500 poṉ varuvāyuḷḷa nāṉku kirāmaṅkaḷiṉ variyi
nīkkik koṭuttuḷḷār. ivar perumāḷi mīṇṭum piratiṭṭi ceyta iṭam muṟkālattil
nantivarmapallavaṉ piratiṭṭi ceytirunta tiṇṇiyaḷavākiya ciṟiya iṭamē eṉpatu iṅkuk
kavaṉittaṟkuriyatākum. āyiṉum iṅku eḻuntaruḷuvitta perumāḷip pūcikka
srīviṣṇavarkaḷi niyamittamiyāl perumāḷi vaḻipāṭu ceyyum urimiyi aracaṉ vaḻiyākap
peṟṟa av viṇavarkaḷ mellamellat taṅkaḷukkuriyaṉavāka naṭarācar kōyiliṭaṅkaḷi, valitiṟ
kippaṟṟikkoḷḷa muyaṉṟu vantārkaḷ. vēṅkaṭapati tēvamakārāyar āṭciyil avaruṭiya
piratinitiyākac ceñciyiliruntu āṭcipurinta viyappa kiruṣṇappa koṇṭama nāyakaṉ eṉṉum
atikāri viṇavar muyaṟcikku uṭantiyāyk ki.pi. 1597-il naṭarācar kōyil mutaṟ
pirākārattilēyē kōvintarācap perumāḷukkut taṉik kōyili amikkat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ.
inniliyil tillip peruṅkōyiliṉ pūciyurimiyit toṉṟutoṭṭup peṟṟuṭiyarākiya
tillivāḻantaṇarkaḷum nakarap potu makkaḷum naṭarācapperumāṉukkēyuriya ikkōyilil
perumāḷukkeṉap putitākat taṉikkōyil kaṭṭutal vēṇṭām eṉavum iraṇṭām pirākārattil
muṉṉirunta iṭattilēyē perumāḷukkup pūci nikaḻac ceytalē poruttamākum eṉavum
avvatikāriyi evvaḷavō muṟi nayantu kēṭṭārkaḷ. koṇṭatu viṭāk koṇṭama nāyakkaṉākiya
avvatikāri avarkaḷatu vēṇṭukōḷip poruṭpaṭuttāmal kōvinta rācar cannitiyip
poṉṉampalalāṇar cannitikku mikavum arukilēyē amikkat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. anniliyil tilli
vāḻantaṇarkaḷ taṅkaḷ uyirik koṭuttēṉum av vaṉ ceyalit taṭuttu niṟutta uṟuti
pūṇṭaṉar; tāṅkaḷ uyirōṭirukkum vari naṭarācar kōyiliṉ uṭpuṟattil kōvintarācap
perumāḷ kōyil kaṭṭutaṟku iṇaṅka māṭṭōm eṉṟu colli oruvar piṉ oruvarākak
kōpurattiṉ mēlēṟik kīḻē vīḻntu uyir viṭuvārāyiṉar. ivvāṟu irupatu pēr vari
taṟkoli ceytu koṇṭa tuṉpakkāṭciyit taṉ kaṇkaḷāṟ kaṇṭum maṉamiraṅkāta koṇṭama nāyakkaṉ
kōpurattiṉ mēlēṟit taṟkoli ceyya muntupavarkaḷiyellām cuṭṭut taḷḷumpaṭi
uttaraviṭṭāṉ. ataṉpaṭi tilli vāḻantaṇar iruvar cuṭappaṭṭu iṟantaṉar. ittuṉpa tiliyip
poṟukkalāṟṟātu tillivāḻantaṇar kuṭumpattic cērnta ammiyār oruvar tamatu
kaḻuttiyaṟuttut taṟkoli ceytu koṇṭār.
ivvāṟu aracāṅka atikāriyoruvaṉatu camayap piṭi vātaṅkāraṇamākac citamparam
kōyilukkuḷḷē ittuṉpac ceyalkaḷ nikaḻnta nāḷil mēli nāṭṭiliruntu yāttiri
kāraṇamākat tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkuc cuṟṟuppayaṇañ ceyta pimeṇṭā (Jesevit Father N.
Pimenta) eṉṉum pātiriyār koṇṭama nāyakkaṉ ceyta ikkoṭuṅ kōṉmiyi nēriṟ kaṇṭu
varuntiyatuṭaṉ. ik koṭuñceyalit tammuṭiya yāttirik kuṟippilum kuṟittuḷḷār.
innikaḻcciyi yuṇarum nallaṟivuṭiyōr evarum kaṇṇīr viṭṭu varuntuvar eṉpatu tiṇṇam.
The Aravidu Dynasty by Father Heras (P. 553)
1. 'A notable instance of the struggle between the two sects is the
lamentable event that took place at Chidambaram in 1597 A. D. While
Krishnappa Nayaka of Jinji, himself a Stunch Vaishnava, was there
Superintending the improvements which he had ordered at the temple of
Govinda Raja within the great Saiva Temple. Father: N. Pimenta, who
passed through Chidambaram at this time narrates in one of his letters
that on this occasion a great controversy arose as to 'Whether it were
lawful to place the signe of Perumal in the temple at Chidambaram. Some
cefused, others by their legates importunatly urged and the Naiks of
Gingee degreed to erect in the temple'. These last words of pimenta
indicate that after the restoration of the idol to the temple by Rama
Rayan it had agin been removed and its shrine probably destroyed. In
order to re-instal it with due honour, Krishnappa Nayaka ordered the old
shrine to be repired and even perhaps enlarged'
This was the cuse of the whole trouble. "The priests of the Temple
which were the Treasuries” Continues, pimenta (were) with standing, and
threatening if it was done, to cast down themselves from the top. The
Brahmanes of the temple Swore to do the like after they buried the
former, which yet after better advice they performed not". But
Krishanppa Nayaka was unmoved by any such threat. When the
reconstruction of the shrine was carried on with out hesitation where
upon the priests climbing one of the high Gopurams of the temple started
to cast themselves down while the Nayak was in the temple. 'Aboat twenty
bad perished in that precipitation on that day of our departure, whereat
the Naichus angry, cused his gunners to shoot at the rest which killed
two of them. A woman also was so hot in this Zealous quarrel taat she
cut her own throat", Naturally Krishnappa Nayaka accomplished his
purpose in spite of this opposition.
nantivarmapallavaṉ tirumāl mūrttattit tillik kōyilil piratiṣṭi ceyta kālattil
attirumēṉi tiṇṇiyaḷavāka amitta ciṟiya iṭattilē tāṉ vaḻipaṭap peṟṟatu. piṉ nāṉūṟu
āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittu accutarāyar piratiṣṭi ceytatum atē iṭattil tāṉ. camayaveṟipiṭitta
viyappa kiruṣṇappa koṇṭama nāyakkaṉ eṉpāṉ ki.pi 1597-il muṉṉirunta at
teṟṟiyampalattaḷavil nillātu naṭarācarcannitimukappiluḷḷa iṭattiyum cērttuk koṇṭu,
kōvinta rācap perumāḷukkut taṉik kōyil kaṭṭiviṭṭāṉ. inta vampukaḷukkellām kāraṇam
viṣṇavatti eṅkum parappa vēṇṭumeṉṟu muyaṉṟa vicayanakara aracarkaḷum avarkaḷukkut tuṇi
niṉṟa avarkaḷuṭiya kuru tātācāriyārum āvar eṉṟu aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakattil
varalāṟṟut tuṟip pērāciriyarāka iruntu kālañceṉṟa C.S.cīṉivācācāriyār avarkaḷ maṉam
varunti eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
kiruṣṇappa nāyakkaṉukkup piṉ vicayanakara maṉṉaṉākiya cīraṅkarāyaṉ-III ki.pi. 1643-il
tillikkōvintarācar cannitiyi mēlum virivupaṭutta eṇṇi muṉpu illāta
puṇṭarīkavallittāyār canniti mutaliya putiya cannitikaḷiyum tillikkōyilil amittāṉ.
ivarkaḷuṭiya mataveṟi kāraṇamākat tilli naṭarācar kōyiliṟ paḻamiyāka irunta cilacannitikaḷ
iṭikkappaṭṭu maṟintupōyiṉa. ivvāṟu viṣṇavarkaḷ koñcaṅ koñcamākat tillikkōyiliṉ
perumpakutiyit tamakku urimiyākkikkoḷḷum muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭuvārāyiṉar.
ataṉiyuṇarnta tillinakarac civap perumakkaḷum tillivāḻantaṇarkaḷum kotitteḻuntu
tillikkōyilil kōvintarācapperumāḷukku iṉi iṭamilliyeṉṟu kūṟum aḷavukkup
perumāḷ cannitiyiyē appuṟappaṭuttum muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭuvārāyiṉar. anniliyil
kōvintarācap perumāḷ pūci muṟiyiṉik kaṇkāṇikkum urimiyuṭiya srīviṣṇavarkaḷ ki.
pi. 1862-il tillip peruṅkōyil pūci muṟi urimiyāḷarākiya' tillivāḻantaṇarkaḷōṭu
ōr uṭaṉpaṭikki ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāmi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. citamparam kōvintarācap
perumāḷ pūci muṟiyik kavaṉikkum urimiyaṭiya viṣṇavarkaḷ tillivāḻantaṇarkaṭku eḻutik
koṭutta uṭaṉpaṭikkiyil tāṅkaḷ kōvintarācap perumāḷukkuc ceytu varum
nittiyapūcikaḷit tavira vēṟu piramōṟcavam naṭattuvatilliyeṉavum, tilliyil
naṭarācapperumāṉukkut toṉṟu toṭṭu naṭipeṟṟu varum nittiya pūcikaḷilum
tiruviḻākkaḷilum tām taṭiyāka iruppatilliyeṉavum oppukkoṇṭu uṟuti
kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ivvuṟutiyiṉ pēril ki. pi. 1867-il nītimaṉṟamum tīrppaḷittuḷḷatu.
tillip peruṅkōyilil civar viṇavar ākiya irutiṟattārkkum ēṟpaṭṭa uṭaṉpāṭum
tillivāḻantaṇarkaḷ peṟṟuḷḷa nītimaṉṟat tīrppum kāraṇamākat tillip peruṅkōyilil
civarum viṇavarkaḷum aṉpiṉāl oṉṟukūṭi naṭarācar kōvintarācar ākiya
iruperunteyvaṅkaḷiyum vaḻipaṭṭu makiḻtaṟkuriya amitiyāṉa cūḻnili toṭarntu
nilipeṟṟuḷḷatu eṉpataṉi ittirukkōyilukku varum aṉparkaḷ ellōrum naṉkuṇarvar.
ittakiya amitiniliyē eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavi iṉpam aḷippatākuka.
